code.gs
const SHEETS = {
  EVENTS:      'ปฏิทินระบบ',
  SETTINGS:    'Settings',
  ADMIN_CAL:   'Admin',      
  TEACHERS_CAL:'TeachersCalendar', 
  TRASH:       'Trash',
};

function doGet(e) {
  _autoSetupIfNeeded(); /* สร้างโฟลเดอร์+sheets อัตโนมัติเมื่อ deploy ครั้งแรก */
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('index')
    .setTitle('ปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน')
    .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1')
    .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
}

function _autoSetupIfNeeded() {
  try {
    var ss     = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    var ssFile = DriveApp.getFileById(ss.getId());
    var parents = ssFile.getParents();

    /* ตรวจว่าไฟล์อยู่ใน folder ชื่อ 'ระบบปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน' แล้วหรือยัง
       ถ้าใช่ = setup แล้ว ข้ามไป */
    if (parents.hasNext()) {
      var currentParent = parents.next();
      if (currentParent.getName() === 'ระบบปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน') return;

      /* ยังไม่ได้อยู่ใน folder ที่ถูกต้อง → หาหรือสร้างโฟลเดอร์ใน parent ปัจจุบัน */
      var existing = currentParent.getFoldersByName('ระบบปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน');
      var mainFolder = existing.hasNext()
        ? existing.next()
        : currentParent.createFolder('ระบบปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน');

      try { ssFile.moveTo(mainFolder); } catch(e2) {}
      _setupCalendarSheets(ss);
      _setupTriggers();

    } else {
      /* ไม่มี parent เลย → สร้างใน root */
      var root = DriveApp.getRootFolder();
      var existing2 = root.getFoldersByName('ระบบปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน');
      var mainFolder2 = existing2.hasNext()
        ? existing2.next()
        : root.createFolder('ระบบปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน');

      try { ssFile.moveTo(mainFolder2); } catch(e2) {}
      _setupCalendarSheets(ss);
      _setupTriggers();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log('_autoSetupIfNeeded error: ' + e.message);
  }
}

function setupSystem() {
  try {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

    // Create folder structure
    var file = DriveApp.getFileById(ss.getId());
    var parents = file.getParents();
    var parentFolder = parents.hasNext() ? parents.next() : DriveApp.getRootFolder();
    var mainFolder = _getOrCreateFolder('ระบบปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน', parentFolder);
    try { mainFolder.addFile(file); parentFolder.removeFile(file); } catch(e) {}

    _setupCalendarSheets(ss);
    _setupTriggers();

    return { success: true, message: '✅ ระบบพร้อมใช้งาน!\n\nรหัสผ่านเริ่มต้น: admin1234\nกรุณาเปลี่ยนรหัสผ่านหลังเข้าระบบ' };
  } catch(err) {
    return { success: false, message: err.message };
  }
}

function _getOrCreateFolder(name, parent) {
  var it = parent.getFoldersByName(name);
  return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : parent.createFolder(name);
}

function _setupCalendarSheets(ss) {
  // ── Events Sheet ──
  var evSh = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.EVENTS);
  if (!evSh) {
    evSh = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var evHeaders = ['id','date','title','description','location','category','color','allDay','notified','createdAt','updatedAt'];
    evSh.getRange(1,1,1,evHeaders.length).setValues([evHeaders])
      .setBackground('#1a1a2e').setFontColor('#fff').setFontWeight('bold');
    evSh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }

  var adSh = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMIN_CAL);
  if (!adSh) {
    adSh = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.ADMIN_CAL);
    adSh.getRange('A1:B1').setValues([['key','value']])
      .setBackground('#c0392b').setFontColor('#fff').setFontWeight('bold');
    adSh.getRange('A2:B2').setValues([['password','admin1234']]);
    adSh.getRange('A3:B3').setValues([['last_login','']]);
    adSh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }

  var stSh = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.SETTINGS);
  if (!stSh) {
    stSh = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.SETTINGS);
    stSh.getRange('A1:B1').setValues([['key','value']])
      .setBackground('#1a1a2e').setFontColor('#fff').setFontWeight('bold');
    stSh.getRange('A2:B2').setValues([['school_name','โรงเรียน...']]);
    stSh.getRange('A3:B3').setValues([['notify_days_before','1']]);
    stSh.getRange('A4:B4').setValues([['telegram_bot_token','']]);
    stSh.getRange('A5:B5').setValues([['telegram_chat_id','']]);
    stSh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }

  var tcSh = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TEACHERS_CAL);
  if (!tcSh) {
    tcSh = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.TEACHERS_CAL);
    var tcHeaders = ['id','name','email','department','active'];
    tcSh.getRange(1,1,1,tcHeaders.length).setValues([tcHeaders])
      .setBackground('#1a1a2e').setFontColor('#fff').setFontWeight('bold');
    tcSh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }

  var trSh = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.TRASH);
  if (!trSh) {
    trSh = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.TRASH);
    var trHeaders = ['id','date','title','description','location','category','color','allDay','notified','createdAt','updatedAt','deletedAt'];
    trSh.getRange(1,1,1,trHeaders.length).setValues([trHeaders])
      .setBackground('#7f8c8d').setFontColor('#fff').setFontWeight('bold');
    trSh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }

  try {
    var d = ss.getSheetByName('Sheet1') || ss.getSheetByName('แผ่น1');
    if (d && ss.getSheets().length > 1) ss.deleteSheet(d);
  } catch(e) {}
}

function _setupTriggers() {
  // ลบ trigger เก่าทั้ง daily และ weekly ก่อน แล้วสร้างใหม่
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function(t) {
    var fn = t.getHandlerFunction();
    if (fn === 'dailyNotification' || fn === 'weeklyDigest') {
      ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t);
    }
  });
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('dailyNotification').timeBased().everyDays(1).atHour(7).create();
  // Weekly digest ทุกวันจันทร์ 7:00 น.
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('weeklyDigest').timeBased().onWeekDay(ScriptApp.WeekDay.MONDAY).atHour(7).create();
}

function getDeployUrl() {
  try {
    return ScriptApp.getService().getUrl();
  } catch(e) {
    return null;
  }
}

function adminLogin(password) {
  try {
    var ss   = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    var tpwd = String(password || '').trim();
    if (!tpwd) return { success: false, error: 'กรุณากรอกรหัสผ่าน' };

    // ── อ่านรหัสจาก Sheet "Admin" B2 ──
    var adSh = ss.getSheetByName('Admin');
    if (!adSh) return { success: false, error: 'ไม่พบชีต Admin' };

    // อ่านทุกแถวในชีต Admin แล้วหา key = "password"
    var data = adSh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var stored = null;
    var rowIdx = -1;
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]).trim().toLowerCase() === 'password') {
        stored = String(data[i][1]).trim();
        rowIdx = i + 1; // 1-based row index
        break;
      }
    }

    // fallback: อ่าน B2 โดยตรง ถ้าหา key ไม่เจอ
    if (stored === null) {
      stored = String(adSh.getRange('B2').getValue()).trim();
      rowIdx = 2;
    }

    if (!stored) return { success: false, error: 'ไม่พบรหัสผ่านในชีต Admin' };
    if (tpwd !== stored) return { success: false, error: 'รหัสผ่านไม่ถูกต้อง' };

    // บันทึกเวลา login ล่าสุด
    try {
      // หา key = "last_login"
      var allData = adSh.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (var j = 1; j < allData.length; j++) {
        if (String(allData[j][0]).trim().toLowerCase() === 'last_login') {
          adSh.getRange(j + 1, 2).setValue(new Date().toISOString());
          break;
        }
      }
    } catch(e) {}

    return { success: true };
  } catch(err) {
    return { success: false, error: err.message };
  }
}

function changePasswordDirect(newPass) {
  try {
    if (!newPass || String(newPass).trim().length < 4)
      return { success: false, error: 'รหัสผ่านต้องมีอย่างน้อย 4 ตัวอักษร' };
    var adSh = _getSheet('Admin');
    if (!adSh) return { success: false, error: 'ไม่พบชีต Admin' };
    var data = adSh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]).trim().toLowerCase() === 'password') {
        adSh.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(String(newPass).trim());
        return { success: true };
      }
    }
    // fallback: เขียน B2
    adSh.getRange('B2').setValue(String(newPass).trim());
    return { success: true };
  } catch(err) { return { success: false, error: err.message }; }
}

function changePassword(oldPass, newPass) {
  try {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    var adSh = ss.getSheetByName('Admin');
    if (!adSh) return { success: false, error: 'ไม่พบชีต Admin' };
    var data = adSh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]).trim().toLowerCase() === 'password') {
        var stored = String(data[i][1]).trim();
        if (oldPass !== stored) return { success: false, error: 'รหัสผ่านเดิมไม่ถูกต้อง' };
        if (!newPass || newPass.length < 4) return { success: false, error: 'รหัสผ่านใหม่ต้องมีอย่างน้อย 4 ตัว' };
        adSh.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(newPass);
        return { success: true };
      }
    }
    // fallback B2
    var cur = String(adSh.getRange('B2').getValue()).trim();
    if (oldPass !== cur) return { success: false, error: 'รหัสผ่านเดิมไม่ถูกต้อง' };
    adSh.getRange('B2').setValue(newPass);
    return { success: true };
  } catch(err) {
    return { success: false, error: err.message };
  }
}

function getInitialData() {
  try {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

    // ── Events ──
    var evSh = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var evData = evSh ? evSh.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
    var events = [];
    if (evData.length > 1) {
      var headers = evData[0].map(function(h){ return String(h).trim(); });
      var tz = 'Asia/Bangkok';
      for (var i = 1; i < evData.length; i++) {
        if (!evData[i][0]) continue;
        var row = evData[i];
        var ev = {};
        for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) {
          var val = row[j];
          ev[headers[j]] = (val instanceof Date)
            ? Utilities.formatDate(val, tz, 'yyyy-MM-dd')
            : val;
        }
        if (ev.date) events.push(ev);
      }
    }

    var stSh = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.SETTINGS);
    var stData = stSh ? stSh.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
    var settings = {};
    for (var si = 1; si < stData.length; si++) {
      if (!stData[si][0]) continue;
      var key = String(stData[si][0]).trim();
      var val2 = stData[si][1];
      settings[key] = (val2 instanceof Date)
        ? Utilities.formatDate(val2, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd')
        : val2;
    }

    events.sort(function(a,b){ return a.date < b.date ? -1 : a.date > b.date ? 1 : 0; });
    return { success: true, events: events, settings: settings };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success: false, error: e.message };
  }
}


function getEvents(year) {
  try {
    var sh = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (data.length <= 1) return { success: true, data: [] };
    var headers = data[0].map(function(h){ return String(h).trim(); });
    var events = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var row = data[i];
      if (!row[0]) continue;
      var ev = {};
      headers.forEach(function(h, idx) {
        var val = row[idx];
        // แปลง Date object → string yyyy-MM-dd
        if (val instanceof Date) {
          ev[h] = Utilities.formatDate(val, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
        } else {
          ev[h] = val;
        }
      });
      // ตรวจว่า date field ถูกต้อง
      if (!ev.date || ev.date === '') continue;
      if (year) {
        var yr = parseInt(String(ev.date).substring(0, 4));
        if (yr !== parseInt(year)) continue;
      }
      events.push(ev);
    }
    return { success: true, data: events };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function getEventsByRange(startDate, endDate) {
  try {
    var sh = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (data.length <= 1) return { success: true, data: [] };
    var headers = data[0].map(function(h){ return String(h).trim(); });
    var start = new Date(startDate); start.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    var end   = new Date(endDate);   end.setHours(23,59,59,999);
    var events = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (!data[i][0]) continue;
      var ev = {};
      headers.forEach(function(h, idx) {
        var val = data[i][idx];
        if (val instanceof Date) {
          ev[h] = Utilities.formatDate(val, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
        } else {
          ev[h] = val;
        }
      });
      if (!ev.date) continue;
      var d = new Date(ev.date);
      if (d >= start && d <= end) events.push(ev);
    }
    return { success: true, data: events };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function addEvent(eventData) {
  try {
    // ถ้า adminPass ว่าง = ข้าม auth (user เข้าระบบมาแล้วผ่าน SPA login)
    if (eventData.adminPass && String(eventData.adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(eventData.adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }

    var sh  = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var id  = 'ev_' + Date.now();
    var now = new Date().toISOString();
    sh.appendRow([
      id, eventData.date, eventData.title, eventData.description || '',
      eventData.location || '', eventData.category || 'ทั่วไป',
      eventData.color || '#4f6ef7', eventData.allDay !== false, false, now, now
    ]);
    _sendEventNotification(eventData);
    return { success: true, id: id };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function updateEvent(eventData) {
  try {
    if (eventData.adminPass && String(eventData.adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(eventData.adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }

    var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]) === String(eventData.id)) {
        var now = new Date().toISOString();
        sh.getRange(i+1, 2, 1, 9).setValues([[
          eventData.date, eventData.title, eventData.description || '',
          eventData.location || '', eventData.category || 'ทั่วไป',
          eventData.color || '#4f6ef7', eventData.allDay !== false, false, now
        ]]);
        return { success: true };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, error: 'ไม่พบกิจกรรม' };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function deleteEvent(id, adminPass) {
  try {
    // ข้าม auth ถ้า adminPass ว่าง
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }

    var sh    = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var trSh  = _getSheet(SHEETS.TRASH);
    var data  = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]) === String(id)) {
        var row = data[i].concat([new Date().toISOString()]);
        trSh.appendRow(row);
        sh.deleteRow(i + 1);
        return { success: true };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, error: 'ไม่พบกิจกรรม' };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function getSettings(adminPass) {
  try {
    var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.SETTINGS);
    if (!sh) return { success: true, data: {} };
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var settings = {};
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (!data[i][0]) continue;
      var key = String(data[i][0]);
      var val = data[i][1];
      /* ถ้าค่าเป็น Date object (Google Sheets แปลงให้อัตโนมัติ)
         ให้แปลงกลับเป็น yyyy-MM-dd string เสมอ */
      if (val instanceof Date) {
        val = Utilities.formatDate(val, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
      }
      settings[key] = val;
    }
    return { success: true, data: settings };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function saveSettings(settings, adminPass) {
  try {
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.SETTINGS);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    Object.keys(settings).forEach(function(key) {
      var val = settings[key];
      /* บันทึก date value เป็น plain string เสมอ ป้องกัน Sheets แปลงเป็น Date */
      var found = false;
      for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        if (String(data[i][0]) === key) {
          sh.getRange(i+1, 2).setValue(val);
          found = true; break;
        }
      }
      if (!found) sh.appendRow([key, val]);
    });
    return { success: true };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function getThaiHolidays(year) {
  try {
    var cal = CalendarApp.getCalendarById('th.th#holiday@group.v.calendar.google.com');
    if (!cal) return { success: true, data: [] };
    var start = new Date(year, 0, 1);
    var end   = new Date(year, 11, 31);
    var evs   = cal.getEvents(start, end);
    var holidays = evs.map(function(e) {
      return {
        date:     Utilities.formatDate(e.getStartTime(), 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd'),
        title:    e.getTitle(),
        category: 'วันหยุดราชการ',
        color:    '#ef4444'
      };
    });
    return { success: true, data: holidays };
  } catch(e) { return { success: true, data: [] }; }
}

function importHolidaysToSheet(holidays, adminPass) {
  try {
    // ข้าม auth ถ้า adminPass ว่าง (login มาแล้ว)
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    if (!holidays || !holidays.length) return { success: true, count: 0 };

    var sh  = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var now = new Date().toISOString();

    // เขียนแบบ batch (setValues ทีเดียว) เร็วกว่า appendRow ทีละแถวมาก
    var baseId = Date.now();
    var rows = holidays.map(function(h, i) {
      var id = 'hol_' + (h.date || '').replace(/-/g,'') + '_' + (baseId + i);
      return [id, h.date, h.title, '', '', 'วันหยุดราชการ', '#ef4444', true, false, now, now];
    });
    var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
    sh.getRange(lastRow + 1, 1, rows.length, 11).setValues(rows);

    return { success: true, count: rows.length };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function emptyTrash(adminPass) {
  try {
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    var sh = _getSheet(SHEETS.TRASH);
    if (!sh) return { success: true, count: 0 };
    var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow <= 1) return { success: true, count: 0 };
    var count = lastRow - 1;
    sh.deleteRows(2, count);
    return { success: true, count: count };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function getTrash(adminPass) {
  try {
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    var sh = _getSheet(SHEETS.TRASH);
    if (!sh) return { success: true, data: [] };
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (data.length <= 1) return { success: true, data: [] };
    var headers = data[0].map(function(h){ return String(h).trim(); });
    var items = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (!data[i][0]) continue;
      var ev = {};
      headers.forEach(function(h, idx) {
        var val = data[i][idx];
        /* แปลง Date object → string */
        if (val instanceof Date) {
          ev[h] = Utilities.formatDate(val, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
        } else {
          ev[h] = val;
        }
      });
      /* deletedAt อาจอยู่ใน column ท้ายสุดที่ไม่มี header */
      if (!ev.deletedAt && data[i].length > headers.length) {
        var lastVal = data[i][data[i].length - 1];
        ev.deletedAt = (lastVal instanceof Date)
          ? Utilities.formatDate(lastVal, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm')
          : String(lastVal || '');
      }
      items.push(ev);
    }
    return { success: true, data: items };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function restoreEvent(id, adminPass) {
  try {
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    var trSh = _getSheet(SHEETS.TRASH);
    var evSh = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var data = trSh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]) === String(id)) {
        evSh.appendRow(data[i].slice(0, 11));
        trSh.deleteRow(i + 1);
        return { success: true };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, error: 'ไม่พบข้อมูล' };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function permanentDelete(id, adminPass) {
  try {
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.TRASH);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]) === String(id)) {
        sh.deleteRow(i + 1);
        return { success: true };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, error: 'ไม่พบข้อมูล' };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function clearYearData(year, type, adminPass) {
  try {
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var trSh = _getSheet(SHEETS.TRASH);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var headers = data[0];
    var keep    = [headers];   /* แถวที่เก็บไว้ */
    var trash   = [];          /* แถวที่ย้ายไปถัง */
    var now     = new Date().toISOString();
    var yr      = parseInt(year);
    var sRange  = type === 'academic' ? new Date(yr, 4, 1) : null;
    var eRange  = type === 'academic' ? new Date(yr+1, 3, 30) : null;

    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (!data[i][0]) { keep.push(data[i]); continue; }
      var d = new Date(data[i][1]);
      var inRange = (type === 'calendar')
        ? (d.getFullYear() === yr)
        : (d >= sRange && d <= eRange);
      if (inRange) {
        trash.push(data[i].concat([now]));
      } else {
        keep.push(data[i]);
      }
    }

    /* ── batch write: เร็วกว่า deleteRow loop มาก ── */
    if (trash.length > 0) {
      /* ย้ายไปถัง */
      trSh.getRange(trSh.getLastRow()+1, 1, trash.length, trash[0].length)
          .setValues(trash);
      /* เขียน events ที่เก็บกลับ */
      sh.clearContents();
      sh.getRange(1, 1, keep.length, keep[0].length).setValues(keep);
    }
    return { success: true, deleted: trash.length };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function exportCalendarData(year, type, adminPass) {
  try {
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    var res;
    if (type === 'academic') {
      var s = new Date(year, 4, 1), e = new Date(parseInt(year)+1, 3, 30);
      res = getEventsByRange(s.toISOString(), e.toISOString());
    } else {
      res = getEvents(year);
    }
    return res;
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function getTeachersCalendar(adminPass) {
  try {
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.TEACHERS_CAL);
    if (!sh) return { success: true, data: [] };
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (data.length <= 1) return { success: true, data: [] };
    var headers = data[0];
    var teachers = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (!data[i][0]) continue;
      var t = {};
      headers.forEach(function(h, idx) { t[h] = data[i][idx]; });
      teachers.push(t);
    }
    return { success: true, data: teachers };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function _getOrCreateTeachersSheet() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sh = (_SS_CACHE||ss).getSheetByName(SHEETS.TEACHERS_CAL);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = (_SS_CACHE||ss).insertSheet(SHEETS.TEACHERS_CAL);
    var headers = ['id','name','email','department','active'];
    sh.getRange(1,1,1,headers.length).setValues([headers])
      .setBackground('#1e2d6b').setFontColor('#fff').setFontWeight('bold');
    sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sh;
}

function addTeacherCalendar(teacher, adminPass) {
  try {
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    var sh = _getOrCreateTeachersSheet(); /* สร้างชีตถ้ายังไม่มี */
    var id = 'tc_' + Date.now();
    sh.appendRow([id, teacher.name, teacher.email || '', teacher.department || '', true]);
    return { success: true, id: id };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function deleteTeacherCalendar(id, adminPass) {
  try {
    if (adminPass && String(adminPass).trim() !== '') {
      var authRes = adminLogin(adminPass);
      if (!authRes.success) return { success: false, error: authRes.error };
    }
    var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.TEACHERS_CAL);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]) === String(id)) {
        sh.deleteRow(i + 1);
        return { success: true };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, error: 'ไม่พบข้อมูล' };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function dailyNotification() {
  try {
    var settings = _getSettingsInternal();
    var notifyDays = parseInt(settings.notify_days_before) || 1;
    var today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    var targets = [];
    for (var d = 0; d <= notifyDays; d++) {
      var dt = new Date(today);
      dt.setDate(dt.getDate() + d);
      targets.push(Utilities.formatDate(dt, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd'));
    }
    var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var headers = data[0];
    var toNotify = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (!data[i][0]) continue;
      var ev = {};
      headers.forEach(function(h, idx) { ev[h] = data[i][idx]; });
      var evDate = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(ev.date), 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
      if (targets.indexOf(evDate) > -1 && !ev.notified) {
        toNotify.push({ row: i+1, event: ev });
      }
    }
    if (!toNotify.length) return;

    var teachers  = _getTeachersInternal();
    var schoolName = settings.school_name || 'โรงเรียน';

    toNotify.forEach(function(item) {
      var ev = item.event;
      var evDateStr = _formatDateThai(new Date(ev.date));

      // Email
      teachers.forEach(function(t) {
        if (!t.email || String(t.email).trim() === '' || !t.active) return;
        try {
          MailApp.sendEmail({
            to: t.email,
            subject: '['+schoolName+'] แจ้งเตือนกิจกรรม: '+ev.title,
            htmlBody: _buildEmailHTML(ev, evDateStr, schoolName)
          });
        } catch(err) { Logger.log('Email error: '+err.message); }
      });

      // Telegram
      var botToken = settings.telegram_bot_token;
      var chatId   = settings.telegram_chat_id;
      if (botToken && String(botToken).trim() !== '') {
        _sendTelegram(botToken, chatId, '📅 *'+schoolName+'*\n\n🔔 *'+ev.title+'*\n📆 '+evDateStr+'\n📍 '+(ev.location||'-')+'\n📝 '+(ev.description||'-'));
      }

      var notifiedCol = headers.indexOf('notified') + 1;
      if (notifiedCol > 0) sh.getRange(item.row, notifiedCol).setValue(true);
    });
  } catch(e) { Logger.log('dailyNotification error: '+e.message); }
}

function _sendEventNotification(eventData) {
  try {
    var settings  = _getSettingsInternal();
    var teachers  = _getTeachersInternal();
    var schoolName = settings.school_name || 'โรงเรียน';
    var evDateStr  = _formatDateThai(new Date(eventData.date));
    teachers.forEach(function(t) {
      if (!t.email || String(t.email).trim() === '' || !t.active) return;
      try {
        MailApp.sendEmail({
          to: t.email,
          subject: '['+schoolName+'] กิจกรรมใหม่: '+eventData.title,
          htmlBody: _buildEmailHTML(eventData, evDateStr, schoolName)
        });
      } catch(err) { Logger.log('Email error: '+err.message); }
    });
  } catch(e) { Logger.log('sendNotification error: '+e.message); }
}

var _SS_CACHE = null;
function _getSheet(name) {
  if (!_SS_CACHE) _SS_CACHE = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  return _SS_CACHE.getSheetByName(name);
}

function _getSettingsInternal() {
  var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.SETTINGS);
  var settings = {};
  if (!sh) return settings;
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][0]) settings[String(data[i][0])] = data[i][1];
  }
  return settings;
}

function _getTeachersInternal() {
  var sh = _getSheet(SHEETS.TEACHERS_CAL);
  if (!sh) return [];
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (data.length <= 1) return [];
  var headers = data[0];
  return data.slice(1).filter(function(r) { return r[0]; }).map(function(r) {
    var t = {};
    headers.forEach(function(h, i) { t[h] = r[i]; });
    return t;
  });
}

function _buildEmailHTML(ev, dateStr, schoolName) {
  var color = ev.color || '#4f6ef7';
  return '<!DOCTYPE html><html><body style="font-family:sans-serif;background:#f0f4ff;padding:20px">'
    +'<div style="max-width:560px;margin:0 auto;background:#fff;border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.1)">'
    +'<div style="background:'+color+';padding:20px;text-align:center">'
    +'<h1 style="color:#fff;margin:0;font-size:18px">'+schoolName+'</h1>'
    +'<p style="color:rgba(255,255,255,.85);margin:4px 0 0;font-size:13px">แจ้งเตือนกิจกรรม</p></div>'
    +'<div style="padding:24px">'
    +'<h2 style="color:#1e2d6b;border-left:4px solid '+color+';padding-left:12px;margin:0 0 16px">'+ev.title+'</h2>'
    +'<p>📆 <strong>วันที่:</strong> '+dateStr+'</p>'
    +(ev.location?'<p>📍 <strong>สถานที่:</strong> '+ev.location+'</p>':'')
    +(ev.description?'<p>📝 <strong>รายละเอียด:</strong> '+ev.description+'</p>':'')
    +'</div>'
    +'<div style="background:#f9f9f9;padding:14px 24px;text-align:center;font-size:12px;color:#999">ส่งอัตโนมัติจากระบบปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน</div>'
    +'</div></body></html>';
}

function _sendTelegram(botToken, chatId, message) {
  if (!botToken || !chatId) return;
  try {
    UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://api.telegram.org/bot'+botToken+'/sendMessage', {
      method: 'post', contentType: 'application/json',
      payload: JSON.stringify({ chat_id: chatId, text: message, parse_mode: 'Markdown' })
    });
  } catch(e) { Logger.log('Telegram error: '+e.message); }
}

function _formatDateThai(date) {
  var days   = ['อาทิตย์','จันทร์','อังคาร','พุธ','พฤหัสบดี','ศุกร์','เสาร์'];
  var months = ['มกราคม','กุมภาพันธ์','มีนาคม','เมษายน','พฤษภาคม','มิถุนายน','กรกฎาคม','สิงหาคม','กันยายน','ตุลาคม','พฤศจิกายน','ธันวาคม'];
  return 'วัน'+days[date.getDay()]+'ที่ '+date.getDate()+' '+months[date.getMonth()]+' '+(date.getFullYear()+543);
}

function weeklyDigest() {
  try {
    var settings   = _getSettingsInternal();
    var teachers   = _getTeachersInternal();
    var schoolName = settings.school_name || 'โรงเรียน';
    // ตรวจ setting weekly_digest_enabled — ถ้าปิดอยู่ให้ข้าม
    if (String(settings.weekly_digest_enabled || 'true').toLowerCase() === 'false') return;

    var today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    // ช่วง 7 วันข้างหน้า (จันทร์ถึงอาทิตย์ของสัปดาห์นี้)
    var weekStart = new Date(today);
    var weekEnd   = new Date(today);
    weekEnd.setDate(weekEnd.getDate() + 6);
    weekEnd.setHours(23,59,59,999);

    var sh      = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var data    = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (data.length <= 1) return;
    var headers = data[0].map(function(h){ return String(h).trim(); });

    var weekEvents = [];
    var wsStr = Utilities.formatDate(weekStart, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
    var weStr = Utilities.formatDate(weekEnd,   'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd');

    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (!data[i][0]) continue;
      var ev = {};
      headers.forEach(function(h, idx) {
        var val = data[i][idx];
        ev[h] = (val instanceof Date)
          ? Utilities.formatDate(val, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd')
          : val;
      });
      if (!ev.date) continue;
      if (ev.date >= wsStr && ev.date <= weStr) weekEvents.push(ev);
    }

    // เรียงตามวันที่
    weekEvents.sort(function(a,b){ return a.date < b.date ? -1 : a.date > b.date ? 1 : 0; });

    var wsLabel = _formatDateThai(weekStart);
    var weLabel = _formatDateThai(weekEnd);
    var subject = '['+schoolName+'] สรุปกิจกรรมประจำสัปดาห์ '+wsLabel+' – '+weLabel;

    teachers.forEach(function(t) {
      if (!t.email || String(t.email).trim() === '' || !t.active) return;
      try {
        MailApp.sendEmail({
          to:       t.email,
          subject:  subject,
          htmlBody: _buildWeeklyHTML(weekEvents, wsLabel, weLabel, schoolName)
        });
      } catch(err) { Logger.log('Weekly email error: '+err.message); }
    });

    // ส่ง Telegram ด้วยถ้าตั้งค่าไว้
    var botToken = settings.telegram_bot_token;
    var chatId   = settings.telegram_chat_id;
    if (botToken && String(botToken).trim() !== '') {
      var tgMsg = '📅 *'+schoolName+'*\n*สรุปกิจกรรมสัปดาห์นี้*\n'+wsLabel+' – '+weLabel+'\n\n';
      if (weekEvents.length === 0) {
        tgMsg += '✅ ไม่มีกิจกรรมในสัปดาห์นี้';
      } else {
        weekEvents.forEach(function(ev) {
          tgMsg += '• '+_formatDateThai(new Date(ev.date))+': *'+ev.title+'*'+(ev.location?' ('+ev.location+')':'')+'\n';
        });
      }
      _sendTelegram(botToken, chatId, tgMsg);
    }
  } catch(e) { Logger.log('weeklyDigest error: '+e.message); }
}

function _buildWeeklyHTML(events, wsLabel, weLabel, schoolName) {
  var rows = '';
  if (events.length === 0) {
    rows = '<tr><td colspan="4" style="padding:20px;text-align:center;color:#94a3b8;font-size:13px">ไม่มีกิจกรรมในสัปดาห์นี้</td></tr>';
  } else {
    events.forEach(function(ev) {
      var color = ev.color || '#4f6ef7';
      var dateStr = '';
      try { dateStr = _formatDateThai(new Date(ev.date)); } catch(e) { dateStr = ev.date; }
      rows += '<tr style="border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0">'
        +'<td style="padding:10px 12px;font-size:13px;color:#666;white-space:nowrap">'+dateStr+'</td>'
        +'<td style="padding:10px 4px"><div style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:'+color+'"></div></td>'
        +'<td style="padding:10px 12px;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:#1e2d6b">'+ev.title+'</td>'
        +'<td style="padding:10px 12px;font-size:12px;color:#94a3b8">'+(ev.location||'')+'</td>'
        +'</tr>';
    });
  }
  return '<!DOCTYPE html><html><body style="font-family:sans-serif;background:#f0f4ff;padding:20px;margin:0">'
    +'<div style="max-width:600px;margin:0 auto;background:#fff;border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.1)">'
    +'<div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4f6ef7,#8b5cf6);padding:24px 28px;text-align:center">'
    +'<div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:800;color:#fff">'+schoolName+'</div>'
    +'<div style="font-size:13px;color:rgba(255,255,255,.8);margin-top:4px">📅 สรุปกิจกรรมประจำสัปดาห์</div>'
    +'<div style="font-size:12px;color:rgba(255,255,255,.65);margin-top:6px">'+wsLabel+' – '+weLabel+'</div>'
    +'</div>'
    +'<div style="padding:0">'
    +'<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse">'+rows+'</table>'
    +'</div>'
    +'<div style="background:#f9f9f9;padding:14px 24px;text-align:center;font-size:12px;color:#999">'
    +'ส่งอัตโนมัติทุกวันจันทร์ · ระบบปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน'
    +'</div>'
    +'</div></body></html>';
}

function getDashboardData() {
  try {
    var settings   = _getSettingsInternal();
    var teachers   = _getTeachersInternal();
    var schoolName = settings.school_name || 'โรงเรียน';

    var today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    var todayStr  = Utilities.formatDate(today, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
    var monthStr  = todayStr.substring(0, 7); // yyyy-MM

    // คำนวณ next 7 days
    var next7 = [];
    for (var di = 0; di <= 6; di++) {
      var nd = new Date(today.getTime() + di * 86400000);
      next7.push(Utilities.formatDate(nd, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd'));
    }

    var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.EVENTS);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var headers = data.length > 0 ? data[0].map(function(h){ return String(h).trim(); }) : [];

    var todayEvents   = [];
    var weekEvents    = [];
    var monthHolidays = 0;

    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (!data[i][0]) continue;
      var ev = {};
      headers.forEach(function(h, idx) {
        var val = data[i][idx];
        ev[h] = (val instanceof Date)
          ? Utilities.formatDate(val, 'Asia/Bangkok', 'yyyy-MM-dd')
          : val;
      });
      if (!ev.date) continue;
      // วันนี้
      if (ev.date === todayStr) todayEvents.push(ev);
      // 7 วัน
      if (next7.indexOf(ev.date) > -1) weekEvents.push(ev);
      // วันหยุดเดือนนี้
      if (ev.date.substring(0,7) === monthStr &&
          (ev.category === 'วันหยุดราชการ' || ev.category === 'วันหยุด')) {
        monthHolidays++;
      }
    }

    // เรียง weekEvents ตามวัน
    weekEvents.sort(function(a,b){ return a.date < b.date ? -1 : a.date > b.date ? 1 : 0; });

    // ดึง 5 กิจกรรมถัดไปจากวันนี้
    var upcoming = weekEvents.filter(function(e){ return e.date >= todayStr; }).slice(0, 5);

    return {
      success:       true,
      schoolName:    schoolName,
      todayCount:    todayEvents.length,
      todayEvents:   todayEvents,
      weekCount:     weekEvents.length,
      monthHolidays: monthHolidays,
      teacherCount:  teachers.length,
      upcoming:      upcoming,
      todayStr:      todayStr,
      weeklyDigestEnabled: String(settings.weekly_digest_enabled || 'true').toLowerCase() !== 'false'
    };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}

function setWeeklyDigest(enabled) {
  try {
    var sh   = _getSheet(SHEETS.SETTINGS);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var found = false;
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]).trim() === 'weekly_digest_enabled') {
        sh.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(enabled ? 'true' : 'false');
        found = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!found) sh.appendRow(['weekly_digest_enabled', enabled ? 'true' : 'false']);
    return { success: true };
  } catch(e) { return { success: false, error: e.message }; }
}
