<script>
/* ── CONSTANTS ── */
var TH_MONTHS=['มกราคม','กุมภาพันธ์','มีนาคม','เมษายน','พฤษภาคม','มิถุนายน','กรกฎาคม','สิงหาคม','กันยายน','ตุลาคม','พฤศจิกายน','ธันวาคม'];
var TH_DAYS=['อาทิตย์','จันทร์','อังคาร','พุธ','พฤหัสบดี','ศุกร์','เสาร์'];
var MCOLS=['#4f6ef7','#0ea5c9','#8b5cf6','#f97316','#10b981','#ec4899','#f59e0b','#ef4444','#14b8a6','#6366f1','#84cc16','#f43f5e'];
var ACOLORS=['#4f6ef7','#3b82f6','#22c55e','#ef4444','#eab308','#f97316','#a855f7','#ec4899','#06b6d4','#84cc16','#f43f5e','#94a3b8'];
var CATCOL={};
CATCOL['ทั่วไป']='#4f6ef7';CATCOL['ประชุม']='#3b82f6';CATCOL['กีฬา']='#22c55e';
CATCOL['วันหยุด']='#ef4444';CATCOL['วันหยุดราชการ']='#ef4444';CATCOL['สอบ']='#eab308';
CATCOL['อบรม']='#f97316';CATCOL['เรียนชดเชย']='#14b8a6';CATCOL['กิจกรรมนักเรียน']='#a855f7';CATCOL['อื่นๆ']='#94a3b8';
var IS_GAS=(typeof google!=='undefined')&&!!google.script;

/* ── PUBLIC STATE ── */
var PUB={
  allEvents:[],calYear:new Date().getFullYear(),calMonth:new Date().getMonth(),
  selectedDate:new Date(),viewMode:'term',displayMode:'events',viewOffset:0,
  planMode:'academic',planOffset:0 /* year plan state */
};
/* ── ADMIN STATE ── */
var ADM={
  allEvents:[],teachers:[],calYear:new Date().getFullYear(),selectedDate:null,
  selectedColor:'#4f6ef7',_newHolidays:[]
};
var exportType='academic',clearType='academic';

/* ════════════════════════════════════════
   INIT
════════════════════════════════════════ */
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){
  updateTodayLabel();
  initTermYearListener();
  if(IS_GAS){
    loadOverlay(true);
    /* ── Speed: รวม 2 calls เป็น 1 (getInitialData) ── */
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
        if(res && res.success){
          PUB.allEvents = (res.events||[]).map(parseEv);
          ADM.allEvents = PUB.allEvents.slice();
          _rebuildPubIdx(); _rebuildAdmIdx(); _rebuildEvById();
          if(res.settings){
            DT_SETTINGS = res.settings;
            if(res.settings.school_name)
              document.getElementById('schoolName').textContent = res.settings.school_name;
          }
        }
        finishPublicLoad();
      })
      .withFailureHandler(function(){ finishPublicLoad(); })
      .getInitialData();
  } else {
    loadDemoData();
    finishPublicLoad();
  }
});

function loadDemoData(){
  var n=new Date(),y=n.getFullYear(),m=n.getMonth();
  function mk(id,day,mo,title,cat,color,loc){
    return{id:id,date:fmtD(new Date(y,mo,day)),title:title,category:cat,color:color,location:loc||'',description:'',allDay:true,_date:new Date(y,mo,day)};
  }
  PUB.allEvents=[
    mk('d1',n.getDate(),m,'ประชุมครูประจำเดือน','ประชุม','#4f6ef7','ห้องประชุมใหญ่'),
    mk('d2',5,m,'กีฬาสี','กีฬา','#10b981','สนามกีฬา'),
    mk('d3',13,m,'วันสงกรานต์','วันหยุดราชการ','#ef4444',''),
    mk('d4',14,m,'วันครอบครัว','วันหยุดราชการ','#ef4444',''),
    mk('d5',15,m,'วันผู้สูงอายุ','วันหยุดราชการ','#ef4444',''),
    mk('d6',18,m,'วันหยุดชดเชยสงกรานต์','วันหยุดราชการ','#ef4444',''),
    mk('d7',20,m,'อบรมหลักสูตรใหม่','อบรม','#f97316','ห้องคอมพิวเตอร์'),
    mk('d8',n.getDate()+2,m,'สอบกลางภาค','สอบ','#f59e0b','ห้องสอบ'),
    mk('d9',1,m+1,'เปิดภาคเรียน','กิจกรรมนักเรียน','#8b5cf6','โรงเรียน')
  ];
  ADM.allEvents=PUB.allEvents.slice();
  document.getElementById('schoolName').textContent='โรงเรียนตัวอย่าง (Preview)';
}

/* ── Public Daytable Year Select ── */
function _setupPubDtYearSelect(){
  var sel = document.getElementById('pubDtYearSelect');
  if(!sel) return;
  if(sel.options.length > 0) return; /* ป้องกัน reset ซ้ำ */
  var n=new Date(), y=n.getFullYear()+543, m=n.getMonth();
  var defYear = (m>=3) ? y : y-1; /* ถ้าเดือน เม.ย.(3)+ ปีการศึกษาปัจจุบัน */
  sel.innerHTML='';
  for(var i=-1; i<=4; i++){
    var o=document.createElement('option');
    o.value=defYear+i;
    o.textContent='ปีการศึกษา '+(defYear+i);
    if(i===0) o.selected=true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  }
}

function onPubDtYearChange(val){
  var yearBE = parseInt(val);
  if(!yearBE) return;
  var prefix = 'ay'+yearBE+'_';
  var t1s = DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term1_start']||'';
  var t1e = DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term1_end']  ||'';
  var t2s = DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term2_start']||'';
  var t2e = DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term2_end']  ||'';
  /* ถ้าไม่มีข้อมูลปีนั้นใน DT_SETTINGS → โหลดจาก server */
  if(IS_GAS && (!t1s||!t2s)){
    loadOverlay(true);
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
        loadOverlay(false);
        if(res&&res.success&&res.data){
          Object.assign(DT_SETTINGS, res.data);
          var p=prefix;
          renderPubDayTable(yearBE,DT_SETTINGS[p+'term1_start']||'',DT_SETTINGS[p+'term1_end']||'',DT_SETTINGS[p+'term2_start']||'',DT_SETTINGS[p+'term2_end']||'');
        } else {
          renderPubDayTable(yearBE,'','','','');
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(function(){ loadOverlay(false); renderPubDayTable(yearBE,'','','',''); })
      .getSettings('');
  } else {
    renderPubDayTable(yearBE, t1s, t1e, t2s, t2e);
  }
}

function finishPublicLoad(){
  loadOverlay(false);
  var n=new Date();
  PUB.selectedDate=n;
  PUB.selectedDateStr=fmtD(n);
  /* render year plan (main public view) */
  setupPlanYearSelect(); /* ← _syncPubDtYearSelect ถูกเรียกใน setupPlanYearSelect แล้ว */
  renderYearPlan();
  /* render pub daytable ถ้ามี settings */
  _setupPubDtYearSelect();
  var yearBE=new Date().getFullYear()+543;
  var prefix='ay'+yearBE+'_';
  var _psel=document.getElementById('pubDtYearSelect');
  if(_psel) _psel.value=yearBE;
  renderPubDayTable(yearBE,DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term1_start']||'',DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term1_end']||'',DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term2_start']||'',DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term2_end']||'');
  setupSearch();
  refreshTodayStatus();
  updateTodayUpcomingChips();
}

/* ════════════════════════════════════════
   PAGE SWITCHING (SPA — ไม่ redirect)
════════════════════════════════════════ */
function showPublicPage(){
  document.getElementById('publicPage').classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('adminPage').classList.remove('active');
  /* refresh public data */
  renderYearPlan();
  refreshTodayStatus();
  updateTodayUpcomingChips();
}

function showAdminPage(){
  document.getElementById('publicPage').classList.remove('active');
  document.getElementById('adminPage').classList.add('active');
  loadAdminData();
}

function loadAdminData(){
  if(IS_GAS){
    /* ถ้า events โหลดมาแล้วตอน init ใช้เลย ไม่โหลดซ้ำ */
    if(ADM.allEvents.length>0){
      renderAdminCalendar();
    } else {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
          if(res&&res.success) ADM.allEvents=(res.data||[]).map(parseEv);
          renderAdminCalendar();
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(){renderAdminCalendar();})
        .getEvents(null);
    }
    /* teachers + settings โหลด parallel เสมอ */
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(res&&res.success){ADM.teachers=res.data||[];renderTeachers();}})
      .withFailureHandler(function(){})
      .getTeachersCalendar('');
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(res&&res.success)loadSettingsForm(res.data);})
      .withFailureHandler(function(){})
      .getSettings('');
  } else {
    ADM.allEvents=PUB.allEvents.slice();
    renderAdminCalendar();renderTeachers();
  }
  setupYearSelects();buildColorPicker();
  updateAdminBadge();
}

/* ════════════════════════════════════════
   LOGIN
════════════════════════════════════════ */
function openLoginModal(){
  document.getElementById('loginPassInput').value='';
  document.getElementById('loginErrMsg').textContent='';
  document.getElementById('loginPassInput').type='password';
  document.getElementById('eyeOpen').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('eyeOff').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('loginModal').classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(function(){document.getElementById('loginPassInput').focus();},120);
}
function closeLoginModal(){document.getElementById('loginModal').classList.remove('show');}
function togglePassEye(){
  var inp=document.getElementById('loginPassInput');
  var open=document.getElementById('eyeOpen'),off=document.getElementById('eyeOff');
  if(inp.type==='password'){inp.type='text';open.style.display='none';off.style.display='block';}
  else{inp.type='password';open.style.display='block';off.style.display='none';}
}
function doAdminLogin(){
  var pass=document.getElementById('loginPassInput').value.trim();
  var errEl=document.getElementById('loginErrMsg');
  errEl.textContent='';
  if(!pass){errEl.textContent='กรุณากรอกรหัสผ่าน';return;}
  if(IS_GAS){
    /* แสดง loading บนปุ่ม + disable input */
    var loginBtn=document.querySelector('.btn-login-go');
    var passInp=document.getElementById('loginPassInput');
    if(loginBtn){
      loginBtn.disabled=true;
      loginBtn.innerHTML='<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px"><span style="display:inline-block;width:16px;height:16px;border:2px solid rgba(255,255,255,.4);border-top-color:white;border-radius:50%;animation:spin .6s linear infinite"></span>กำลังตรวจสอบ...</span>';
    }
    if(passInp) passInp.disabled=true;

    function _resetBtn(){
      if(loginBtn){ loginBtn.disabled=false; loginBtn.innerHTML='🔓 เข้าสู่ระบบ'; }
      if(passInp){ passInp.disabled=false; passInp.focus(); }
    }

    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
        if(res&&res.success){
          _resetBtn();
          closeLoginModal();
          showToast('เข้าสู่ระบบสำเร็จ','success');
          showAdminPage();
        } else {
          _resetBtn();
          errEl.textContent=(res&&res.error)||'รหัสผ่านไม่ถูกต้อง';
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(function(err){
        _resetBtn();
        errEl.textContent='เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: '+(err.message||err);
      })
      .adminLogin(pass);
  } else {
    if(pass.length>=4){
      closeLoginModal();
      showToast('Preview: เข้าสู่ระบบสำเร็จ','success');
      showAdminPage();
    } else {
      errEl.textContent='รหัสผ่านต้องมีอย่างน้อย 4 ตัวอักษร';
    }
  }
}

/* ════════════════════════════════════════
   PUBLIC: CALENDAR
════════════════════════════════════════ */
function renderCalendar(){
  var y=PUB.calYear,m=PUB.calMonth;
  document.getElementById('calMonthLbl').textContent=TH_MONTHS[m]+' '+(y+543);
  var firstDay=new Date(y,m,1).getDay(),daysInM=new Date(y,m+1,0).getDate(),prevDays=new Date(y,m,0).getDate();
  var today=new Date();today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  var todayStr=fmtD(today);
  var frag=document.createDocumentFragment();
  for(var i=firstDay-1;i>=0;i--){var d=document.createElement('div');d.className='cal-day other';d.innerHTML='<span class="cal-day-num">'+(prevDays-i)+'</span>';frag.appendChild(d);}
  for(var day=1;day<=daysInM;day++){
    var ds=y+'-'+('0'+(m+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+day).slice(-2);
    var evs=getPubEvs(ds);
    var isToday=(ds===todayStr);
    var isSel=(PUB.selectedDateStr===ds)&&!isToday;
    var isHol=evs.some(function(e){return e.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||e.category==='วันหยุด';});
    var dow=new Date(y,m,day).getDay();
    var cls='cal-day';
    if(isToday)cls+=' today';else if(isSel)cls+=' selected';
    if(isHol)cls+=' holiday';else if(dow===0)cls+=' is-sun';else if(dow===6)cls+=' is-sat';
    var dots=evs.slice(0,3).map(function(e){return '<div class="cal-day-dot" style="background:'+(e.color||'#4f6ef7')+'"></div>';}).join('');
    var d2=document.createElement('div');d2.className=cls;
    d2.innerHTML='<span class="cal-day-num">'+day+'</span><div class="cal-day-dots">'+dots+'</div>';
    d2.addEventListener('click',(function(dstr,dy,dm,dd){return function(){
      PUB.selectedDateStr=dstr;
      PUB.selectedDate=new Date(dy,dm,dd);
      renderCalendar();renderSelDay(PUB.selectedDate);
    };})(ds,y,m,day));
    frag.appendChild(d2);
  }
  var total=Math.ceil((firstDay+daysInM)/7)*7;
  for(var ki=1;ki<=total-firstDay-daysInM;ki++){var d3=document.createElement('div');d3.className='cal-day other';d3.innerHTML='<span class="cal-day-num">'+ki+'</span>';frag.appendChild(d3);}
  var cont=document.getElementById('calDays');cont.innerHTML='';cont.appendChild(frag);
}
function calNav(dir){PUB.calMonth+=dir;if(PUB.calMonth>11){PUB.calMonth=0;PUB.calYear++;}if(PUB.calMonth<0){PUB.calMonth=11;PUB.calYear--;}renderCalendar();renderMonthEvList(PUB.calYear,PUB.calMonth);}
function calGoToday(){var n=new Date();PUB.calYear=n.getFullYear();PUB.calMonth=n.getMonth();selectDate(n);}
function selectDate(date){
  PUB.selectedDate=date;
  PUB.selectedDateStr=fmtD(date); /* cache string เพื่อใช้ใน render */
  renderCalendar();renderSelDay(date);
}

function renderSelDay(date){
  if(!date)return;
  var ds=fmtD(date),evs=getPubEvs(ds);
  var dow=date.getDay();
  document.getElementById('selDayLbl').textContent='วัน'+TH_DAYS[dow]+'ที่ '+date.getDate()+' '+TH_MONTHS[date.getMonth()]+' '+(date.getFullYear()+543);
  var chips=document.getElementById('selDayChips');
  var isHol=evs.some(function(e){return e.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||e.category==='วันหยุด';});
  if(!evs.length){
    var msg=dow===0?'วันอาทิตย์ ไม่มีกิจกรรม':isHol?'วันหยุด':'ไม่มีกิจกรรม';
    chips.innerHTML='<span style="font-size:.82rem;color:var(--text3)">'+msg+'</span>';return;
  }
  chips.innerHTML=evs.map(function(ev){
    return '<span class="sel-day-ev-chip" style="background:'+(ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+'18;color:'+(ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+';border-color:'+(ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+'30" onclick="openEventModal(\''+ev.id+'\')">'+ev.title+'</span>';
  }).join('');
}

function renderMonthEvList(y,m){
  var prefix=y+'-'+('0'+(m+1)).slice(-2)+'-';
  var evs=PUB.allEvents.filter(function(e){return e.date&&e.date.indexOf(prefix)===0;});
  document.getElementById('monthEvTitle').textContent='กิจกรรม '+TH_MONTHS[m]+' '+(y+543);
  document.getElementById('monthEvCount').textContent=evs.length;
  var body=document.getElementById('monthEvBody');
  if(!evs.length){body.innerHTML='<div class="no-month-ev">ไม่มีกิจกรรมในเดือนนี้</div>';return;}
  /* เรียงตามวัน */
  evs=evs.slice().sort(function(a,b){return a.date<b.date?-1:a.date>b.date?1:0;});
  body.innerHTML=evs.map(function(ev){
    var day=parseInt((ev.date||'').split('-')[2])||'?';
    return '<div class="month-ev-row" onclick="openEventModal(\''+ev.id+'\')">'
      +'<div class="month-ev-dot" style="background:'+(ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+'"></div>'
      +'<div class="month-ev-date">'+day+' '+TH_MONTHS[m].substring(0,3)+'.</div>'
      +'<div class="month-ev-name">'+ev.title+'</div>'
      +'<span style="font-size:.65rem;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:8px;flex-shrink:0;background:'+(ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+'18;color:'+(ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+'">'+ev.category+'</span>'
      +'</div>';
  }).join('');
}

function updateTodayLabel(){
  var n=new Date();
  var dateText='วัน'+TH_DAYS[n.getDay()]+'ที่ '+n.getDate()+' '+TH_MONTHS[n.getMonth()]+' พ.ศ. '+(n.getFullYear()+543);
  document.getElementById('todayDateLbl').innerHTML='<a href="https://www.facebook.com/Treetased.Traiyotee" target="_blank" style="color:inherit;text-decoration:none;">'+dateText+'</a>';
}
function refreshTodayStatus(){
  var todayStr=fmtD(new Date());
  var evs=getPubEvs(todayStr);
  var dow=new Date().getDay();
  var isHol=evs.some(function(e){return e.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||e.category==='วันหยุด';})||dow===0;
  var txt=isHol?'วันหยุด 🎉':evs.length?'มี '+evs.length+' กิจกรรมวันนี้ — คลิกเพื่อดู':'วันทำงานปกติ';
  var lbl=document.getElementById('todayStatusLbl');
  /* ถ้ามีกิจกรรม ให้คลิกดูได้ / ทุกกรณีใส่ลิงก์ Facebook ไว้ด้วย */
  if(evs.length){
    lbl.innerHTML='<a href="https://www.facebook.com/Treetased.Traiyotee" target="_blank" style="color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-color:rgba(255,255,255,.5);">'+txt+'</a>';
    lbl.style.cursor='pointer';
    lbl.onclick=function(){ openPubDayModal(todayStr,evs.map(function(e){return e.id;}).join(',')); };
  } else {
    lbl.innerHTML='<a href="https://www.facebook.com/Treetased.Traiyotee" target="_blank" style="color:inherit;text-decoration:none;">'+txt+'</a>';
    lbl.style.cursor='';
    lbl.onclick=null;
  }
}

function updateStats(){
  var n=new Date();
  var y=n.getFullYear(),m=n.getMonth();
  var monthPrefix=y+'-'+('0'+(m+1)).slice(-2)+'-';
  var yearPrefix=y+'-';
  /* next 7 days */
  var next7Strs={};
  for(var di=0;di<=7;di++){
    next7Strs[fmtD(new Date(n.getTime()+di*86400000))]=true;
  }
  /* ใช้ _pubIdx keys แทน loop ทุก event */
  var mC=0,hC=0,yC=0,up=0;
  Object.keys(_pubIdx).forEach(function(ds){
    var evs=_pubIdx[ds];
    if(ds.indexOf(monthPrefix)===0){
      mC+=evs.length;
      evs.forEach(function(ev){ if(ev.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||ev.category==='วันหยุด') hC++; });
    }
    if(ds.indexOf(yearPrefix)===0) yC+=evs.length;
    if(next7Strs[ds]) up+=evs.length;
  });
  var s1=document.getElementById('st1'),s2=document.getElementById('st2'),
      s3=document.getElementById('st3'),s4=document.getElementById('st4');
  if(s1) s1.textContent=mC;
  if(s2) s2.textContent=hC;
  if(s3) s3.textContent=yC;
  if(s4) s4.textContent=up;
  refreshTodayStatus();
}

/* ── VIEW ── */
function switchView(mode,el){PUB.viewMode=mode;PUB.viewOffset=0;document.querySelectorAll('.view-tabs .view-tab').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('active');});el.classList.add('active');renderView();}
function switchMode(mode,el){PUB.displayMode=mode;var mg=el.closest('.view-tabs');mg.querySelectorAll('.view-tab').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('active');});el.classList.add('active');renderView();}
function viewNav(dir){PUB.viewOffset+=dir;renderView();}

function renderView(){
  var n=new Date(),cy=n.getFullYear(),cm=n.getMonth(),months=[],label='';
  if(PUB.viewMode==='term'){
    var ty=cy,term=(cm>=4&&cm<=9)?1:2;term+=PUB.viewOffset;
    while(term>2){term-=2;ty++;}while(term<1){term+=2;ty--;}
    if(term===1){months=mkMonths(ty,4,6);label='ภาคเรียนที่ 1 ปีการศึกษา '+(ty+543);}
    else{months=mkMonths(ty,10,2).concat(mkMonths(ty+1,0,4));label='ภาคเรียนที่ 2 ปีการศึกษา '+(ty+543);}
  } else if(PUB.viewMode==='academic'){
    var ay=cy+PUB.viewOffset;months=mkMonths(ay,4,8).concat(mkMonths(ay+1,0,4));label='ปีการศึกษา '+(ay+543);
  } else {
    var yr=cy+PUB.viewOffset;months=mkMonths(yr,0,12);label='ปี พ.ศ. '+(yr+543);
  }
  document.getElementById('viewPeriodLbl').textContent=label;
  var grid=document.getElementById('timelineGrid');
  grid.className=PUB.displayMode==='calendar'?'cal-grid-view':'timeline-grid';
  var frag=document.createDocumentFragment();
  var todayStr2=fmtD(new Date());
  months.forEach(function(mo,idx){
    var prefix=mo.y+'-'+('0'+(mo.m+1)).slice(-2)+'-';
    var evs=PUB.allEvents.filter(function(e){return e.date&&e.date.indexOf(prefix)===0;});
    evs=evs.slice().sort(function(a,b){return a.date<b.date?-1:a.date>b.date?1:0;});
    var col=MCOLS[mo.m%12];
    if(PUB.displayMode==='events'){
      var block=document.createElement('div');block.className='month-block';block.style.animation='fadeInUp .3s ease '+(idx*.03)+'s both';
      var rows=evs.slice(0,8).map(function(ev){
        var day=parseInt((ev.date||'').split('-')[2])||'?';
        return '<div class="mb-ev-row" onclick="openEventModal(\''+ev.id+'\')">'
          +'<div class="mb-ev-dot" style="background:'+(ev.color||col)+'"></div>'
          +'<div class="mb-ev-date">'+day+' '+TH_MONTHS[mo.m].substring(0,3)+'.</div>'
          +'<div class="mb-ev-title">'+ev.title+'</div></div>';
      }).join('')||'<div class="no-mb-ev">ไม่มีกิจกรรม</div>';
      block.innerHTML='<div class="month-block-hd mc-'+mo.m%12+'"><span style="font-size:.8rem;font-weight:800;color:white">'+TH_MONTHS[mo.m]+' '+(mo.y+543)+'</span>'+(evs.length?'<span class="month-block-count" style="background:rgba(0,0,0,.2)">'+evs.length+'</span>':'')+'</div><div class="month-block-body">'+rows+(evs.length>8?'<div style="padding:5px 7px;font-size:.7rem;color:'+col+';cursor:pointer;font-weight:700" onclick="jumpToMonth('+mo.y+','+mo.m+')">+ อีก '+(evs.length-8)+' รายการ →</div>':'')+'</div>';
      frag.appendChild(block);
    } else {
      var mini=document.createElement('div');mini.className='mini-month';mini.style.animation='fadeInUp .3s ease '+(idx*.03)+'s both';
      var firstDay=new Date(mo.y,mo.m,1).getDay();
      var dim=new Date(mo.y,mo.m+1,0).getDate();
      var cells='';var prevD=new Date(mo.y,mo.m,0).getDate();
      for(var pi=firstDay-1;pi>=0;pi--) cells+='<div class="mini-day-cell md-other">'+(prevD-pi)+'</div>';
      for(var dd=1;dd<=dim;dd++){
        var dds=mo.y+'-'+('0'+(mo.m+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+dd).slice(-2);
        var devs=getPubEvs(dds);
        var isT=(dds===todayStr2);
        var isH2=devs.some(function(e){return e.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||e.category==='วันหยุด';});
        var dow2=new Date(mo.y,mo.m,dd).getDay();var cls2='mini-day-cell';
        if(isT)cls2+=' md-today';
        if(isH2&&!isT)cls2+=' md-holiday';else if(dow2===0&&!isT)cls2+=' md-sun';else if(dow2===6&&!isT)cls2+=' md-sat';
        var dotEl=devs.length?'<div class="mini-day-dot-bar" style="background:'+(devs[0].color||col)+'"></div>':'';
        cells+='<div class="'+cls2+'" onclick="jumpToMonthDay('+mo.y+','+mo.m+','+dd+')" title="'+devs.map(function(e){return e.title;}).join(', ')+'"><span>'+dd+'</span>'+dotEl+'</div>';
      }
      var tot=Math.ceil((firstDay+dim)/7)*7;for(var ni=1;ni<=tot-firstDay-dim;ni++) cells+='<div class="mini-day-cell md-other">'+ni+'</div>';
      mini.innerHTML='<div class="mini-month-hd mc-'+mo.m%12+'"><span style="font-size:.8rem;font-weight:800;color:white">'+TH_MONTHS[mo.m]+' '+(mo.y+543)+'</span>'+(evs.length?'<span class="mini-month-count">'+evs.length+'</span>':'')+'</div><div class="mini-month-body"><div class="mini-weekdays-row"><div class="mini-wd-cell">อ</div><div class="mini-wd-cell">จ</div><div class="mini-wd-cell">อ</div><div class="mini-wd-cell">พ</div><div class="mini-wd-cell">พฤ</div><div class="mini-wd-cell">ศ</div><div class="mini-wd-cell">ส</div></div><div class="mini-days-grid">'+cells+'</div></div>';
      frag.appendChild(mini);
    }
  });
  grid.innerHTML='';grid.appendChild(frag);
}
function mkMonths(y,startM,count){var r=[];for(var i=0;i<count;i++){var m=startM+i;var yr=y;while(m>11){m-=12;yr++;}r.push({y:yr,m:m});}return r;}
function jumpToMonth(y,m){PUB.calYear=y;PUB.calMonth=m;renderCalendar();renderMonthEvList(y,m);document.getElementById('publicPage').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});}
function jumpToMonthDay(y,m,day){
  PUB.calYear=y;PUB.calMonth=m;
  PUB.selectedDateStr=y+'-'+('0'+(m+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+day).slice(-2);
  PUB.selectedDate=new Date(y,m,day);
  renderCalendar();renderSelDay(PUB.selectedDate);renderMonthEvList(y,m);
  document.getElementById('publicPage').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});
}

/* ── SEARCH ── */
function setupSearch(){
  var inp=document.getElementById('searchInput'),res=document.getElementById('searchResults'),t;
  inp.addEventListener('input',function(){
    clearTimeout(t);t=setTimeout(function(){
      var q=inp.value.trim().toLowerCase();
      if(!q){res.classList.remove('show');return;}
      var found=PUB.allEvents.filter(function(e){return (e.title||'').toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1||(e.location||'').toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1;}).slice(0,8);
      res.innerHTML=found.length?found.map(function(ev){
        var parts=(ev.date||'').split('-');
        var dateLabel=parts.length===3?(parseInt(parts[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(parts[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(parts[0])+543)):ev.date;
        return '<div class="search-result-item" onclick="handleSearch(\''+ev.id+'\')">'
          +'<div style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:'+(ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+';flex-shrink:0"></div>'
          +'<div><div style="font-size:.8rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--text)">'+ev.title+'</div>'
          +'<div style="font-size:.68rem;color:var(--text3)">'+dateLabel+'</div></div></div>';
      }).join(''):'<div style="padding:12px;text-align:center;color:var(--text3);font-size:.75rem">ไม่พบผลลัพธ์</div>';
      res.classList.add('show');
    },200);
  });
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(!e.target.closest('.search-wrap'))res.classList.remove('show');});
}
function handleSearch(id){
  var ev=getEvById(id);if(!ev)return;
  var parts=(ev.date||'').split('-');
  var y=parseInt(parts[0]),m=parseInt(parts[1])-1,d=parseInt(parts[2]);
  PUB.calYear=y;PUB.calMonth=m;
  PUB.selectedDateStr=ev.date;
  PUB.selectedDate=new Date(y,m,d);
  renderCalendar();renderSelDay(PUB.selectedDate);renderMonthEvList(y,m);
  document.getElementById('searchResults').classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('searchInput').value='';
}

/* ── EVENT MODAL (public) ── */
function openEventModal(id){
  var ev=getEvById(id);if(!ev)return;
  var color=ev.color||'#4f6ef7';
  document.getElementById('modalColorBar').style.background=color;
  document.getElementById('modalCatBadge').innerHTML='<span style="background:'+color+'18;color:'+color+';padding:4px 12px;border-radius:14px;font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700">'+(ev.category||'กิจกรรม')+'</span>';
  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent=ev.title;
  /* แปลง date string เป็น Thai */
  var parts=(ev.date||'').split('-');
  var dateLabel=parts.length===3?(parseInt(parts[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(parts[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(parts[0])+543)):ev.date;
  var rows=[{icon:'📆',lbl:'วันที่',val:dateLabel}];
  if(ev.location)rows.push({icon:'📍',lbl:'สถานที่',val:ev.location});
  if(ev.allDay||ev.allDay==='TRUE'||ev.allDay===true)rows.push({icon:'🌅',lbl:'เวลา',val:'ทั้งวัน'});
  if(ev.description)rows.push({icon:'📝',lbl:'รายละเอียด',val:ev.description});
  document.getElementById('modalBody').innerHTML=rows.map(function(r){
    return '<div class="modal-info-row"><span class="modal-info-icon">'+r.icon+'</span><div><div class="modal-info-lbl">'+r.lbl+'</div><div class="modal-info-val">'+r.val+'</div></div></div>';
  }).join('');
  showModal('eventModal');
}

/* ════════════════════════════════════════
   ADMIN: CALENDAR
════════════════════════════════════════ */
/* ── ADMIN CALENDAR VIEW TOGGLE ── */
var ADM_VIEW_MODE = 'year'; /* 'year' | 'month' */
var ADM_VIEW_MONTH = new Date().getMonth(); /* เดือนที่แสดงตอน month mode */

function toggleAdminCalView() {
  if (ADM_VIEW_MODE === 'year') {
    ADM_VIEW_MODE = 'month';
    ADM_VIEW_MONTH = ADM.selectedDate
      ? parseInt((ADM.selectedDate||'').split('-')[1]||new Date().getMonth()+1) - 1
      : new Date().getMonth();
    document.getElementById('adminYearView').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('adminMonthView').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('calViewToggleBtn').textContent = '📆 รายปี';
    renderAdminMonthView();
  } else {
    ADM_VIEW_MODE = 'year';
    document.getElementById('adminYearView').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('adminMonthView').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('calViewToggleBtn').textContent = '📅 รายเดือน';
    renderAdminCalendar();
  }
}

function adminCalNav(dir) {
  if (ADM_VIEW_MODE === 'month') {
    ADM_VIEW_MONTH += dir;
    if (ADM_VIEW_MONTH > 11) { ADM_VIEW_MONTH = 0; ADM.calYear++; }
    if (ADM_VIEW_MONTH < 0)  { ADM_VIEW_MONTH = 11; ADM.calYear--; }
    renderAdminMonthView();
  } else {
    ADM.calYear += dir;
    renderAdminCalendar();
  }
}

function renderAdminMonthView() {
  var y = ADM.calYear, m = ADM_VIEW_MONTH;
  document.getElementById('adminCalTitle').textContent = TH_MONTHS[m] + ' พ.ศ. ' + (y + 543);
  var firstDay = new Date(y, m, 1).getDay();
  var dim = new Date(y, m + 1, 0).getDate();
  var prevDays = new Date(y, m, 0).getDate();
  var todayStr = fmtD(new Date());
  var frag = document.createDocumentFragment();

  /* prev fillers */
  for (var i = firstDay - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    var d = document.createElement('div');
    d.className = 'admin-month-day adm-other';
    d.innerHTML = '<span class="adm-day-num">' + (prevDays - i) + '</span>';
    frag.appendChild(d);
  }
  /* current month */
  for (var day = 1; day <= dim; day++) {
    var ds = y + '-' + ('0'+(m+1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0'+day).slice(-2);
    var evs = getAdmEvs(ds);
    var isToday = (ds === todayStr);
    var isSel = (ADM.selectedDate === ds);
    var isHol = evs.some(function(e){ return e.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||e.category==='วันหยุด'; });
    var dow = new Date(y, m, day).getDay();
    var cls = 'admin-month-day';
    if (isToday) cls += ' adm-today';
    else if (isSel) cls += ' adm-selected';
    if (isHol && !isToday) cls += ' adm-hol';
    if (dow === 0) cls += ' adm-sun';
    if (dow === 6) cls += ' adm-sat';

    /* สร้าง pills ของกิจกรรม */
    var evHtml = '';
    var maxShow = 3;
    if (evs.length) {
      evHtml = '<div class="adm-ev-list">';
      for (var ei = 0; ei < Math.min(evs.length, maxShow); ei++) {
        var ev = evs[ei];
        var c = ev.color || '#4f6ef7';
        var textColor = isToday ? 'rgba(255,255,255,.85)' : c;
        var bgColor = isToday ? 'rgba(255,255,255,.2)' : c + '20';
        evHtml += '<div class="adm-ev-pill" style="background:' + bgColor + ';color:' + textColor + '">' + ev.title + '</div>';
      }
      if (evs.length > maxShow) {
        evHtml += '<div class="adm-ev-more">+' + (evs.length - maxShow) + ' อื่นๆ</div>';
      }
      evHtml += '</div>';
    }

    var d2 = document.createElement('div');
    d2.className = cls;
    d2.title = evs.map(function(e){ return e.title; }).join(', ') || ('วันที่ ' + day);
    d2.innerHTML = '<span class="adm-day-num">' + day + '</span>' + evHtml;
    d2.addEventListener('click', (function(dstr){ return function(){ selectAdminDate(dstr); }; })(ds));
    d2.addEventListener('dblclick', (function(dstr){ return function(){ ADM.selectedDate=dstr; openAddEvent(); }; })(ds));
    d2.title = (evs.length ? evs.map(function(e){return e.title;}).join(', ') : 'ดับเบิลคลิกเพื่อเพิ่มกิจกรรม');
    frag.appendChild(d2);
  }
  /* next fillers */
  var total = Math.ceil((firstDay + dim) / 7) * 7;
  for (var ni = 1; ni <= total - firstDay - dim; ni++) {
    var d3 = document.createElement('div');
    d3.className = 'admin-month-day adm-other';
    d3.innerHTML = '<span class="adm-day-num">' + ni + '</span>';
    frag.appendChild(d3);
  }
  var cont = document.getElementById('adminMonthDays');
  cont.innerHTML = '';
  cont.appendChild(frag);
}

/* ── Admin badge: กิจกรรมใน 7 วัน ── */
function updateAdminBadge() {
  var todayStr = fmtD(new Date());
  var next7 = {};
  for (var di = 0; di <= 6; di++) {
    var nd = new Date(new Date().getTime() + di * 86400000);
    next7[fmtD(nd)] = true;
  }
  var upcoming = ADM.allEvents.filter(function(e){ return next7[e.date]; });
  var todayEvs = ADM.allEvents.filter(function(e){ return e.date === todayStr; });
  var badge = document.getElementById('adminUpcomingBadge');
  if (!badge) return;
  if (upcoming.length) {
    var txt = '';
    if (todayEvs.length) txt = '🔴 วันนี้ ' + todayEvs.length + ' กิจกรรม';
    else txt = '⏰ ใน 7 วัน: ' + upcoming.length + ' กิจกรรม';
    badge.textContent = txt;
    badge.style.display = 'block';
  } else {
    badge.style.display = 'none';
  }
  /* upcoming hint ใน quick panel */
  var hint = document.getElementById('upcomingHint');
  if (hint) {
    var next = ADM.allEvents.filter(function(e){ return e.date >= todayStr; })
      .sort(function(a,b){ return a.date < b.date ? -1 : a.date > b.date ? 1 : 0; })[0];
    if (next) {
      var dp = next.date.split('-');
      var dl = parseInt(dp[2]) + ' ' + TH_MONTHS[parseInt(dp[1])-1] + ' ' + (parseInt(dp[0])+543);
      hint.innerHTML = '📌 กิจกรรมถัดไป: <strong>' + next.title + '</strong> (' + dl + ')';
    } else {
      hint.innerHTML = '';
    }
  }
}

function renderAdminCalendar() {
  document.getElementById('adminCalTitle').textContent = 'ปี พ.ศ. ' + (ADM.calYear + 543);
  var grid=document.getElementById('yearMonthsGrid'),frag=document.createDocumentFragment();
  var today=new Date();today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  for(var m=0;m<12;m++){
    var firstDay=new Date(ADM.calYear,m,1).getDay(),dim=new Date(ADM.calYear,m+1,0).getDate(),prev=new Date(ADM.calYear,m,0).getDate();
    var cells='';
    for(var i=firstDay-1;i>=0;i--) cells+='<div class="mini-day md-other">'+(prev-i)+'</div>';
    for(var d=1;d<=dim;d++){
      var dt=new Date(ADM.calYear,m,d),ds=fmtD(dt),evs=getAdmEvs(ds);
      var isT=(dt.getTime()===today.getTime());
      var isSel=(ADM.selectedDate===ds);
      var isH=evs.some(function(e){return e.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||e.category==='วันหยุด';});
      var dow=dt.getDay();
      var cls='mini-day';
      if(isT) cls+=' md-today';
      else if(isSel) cls+=' md-selected';
      if(isH&&!isT&&!isSel) cls+=' md-hol';
      else if(dow===0&&!isT&&!isSel) cls+=' md-sun';
      else if(dow===6&&!isT&&!isSel) cls+=' md-sat';
      if(evs.length) cls+=' md-ev';
      cells+='<div class="'+cls+'" onclick="selectAdminDate(\''+ds+'\')" ondblclick="adminDayDblClick(\''+ds+'\')" title="ดับเบิลคลิกเพื่อเพิ่มกิจกรรม | '+(evs.map(function(e){return e.title;}).join(', ')||('วันที่ '+d))+'">'+d+'</div>';
    }
    var tot=Math.ceil((firstDay+dim)/7)*7;for(var k=1;k<=tot-firstDay-dim;k++) cells+='<div class="mini-day md-other">'+k+'</div>';
    var block=document.createElement('div');block.className='mini-cal';
    block.innerHTML='<div class="mini-cal-hd" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="switchToAdminMonth('+m+')" title="คลิกเพื่อดูรายเดือน">'+TH_MONTHS[m]+' <span style="font-size:9px;opacity:.6">▶</span></div><div class="mini-cal-grid"><div class="mini-wds"><div class="mini-wd">อ</div><div class="mini-wd">จ</div><div class="mini-wd">อ</div><div class="mini-wd">พ</div><div class="mini-wd">พฤ</div><div class="mini-wd">ศ</div><div class="mini-wd">ส</div></div><div class="mini-days">'+cells+'</div></div>';
    frag.appendChild(block);
  }
  grid.innerHTML='';grid.appendChild(frag);
}
function switchToAdminMonth(m) {
  ADM_VIEW_MONTH = m;
  ADM_VIEW_MODE = 'month';
  document.getElementById('adminYearView').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('adminMonthView').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('calViewToggleBtn').textContent = '📆 รายปี';
  renderAdminMonthView();
}
function selectAdminDate(ds){
  ADM.selectedDate=ds;
  if(ADM_VIEW_MODE==='month') renderAdminMonthView();
  var parts=ds.split('-');
  var d=new Date(parseInt(parts[0]),parseInt(parts[1])-1,parseInt(parts[2]));
  var label=d.getDate()+' '+TH_MONTHS[d.getMonth()]+' '+(d.getFullYear()+543);
  document.getElementById('quickPanelTitle').textContent=label;
  document.getElementById('quickDateLbl').textContent=label;
  document.getElementById('btnAddEvent').style.display='flex';
  renderQuickEvents(ds);
}
/* Double-click วันใน Admin Calendar → เปิด modal เพิ่มกิจกรรมทันที */
function adminDayDblClick(ds){
  ADM.selectedDate=ds;
  openAddEvent();
}
function renderQuickEvents(ds){
  var evs=getAdmEvs(ds),cont=document.getElementById('quickEvents');
  if(!evs.length){cont.innerHTML='<div style="padding:20px;text-align:center;font-size:12px;color:var(--text3)">ไม่มีกิจกรรม</div>';return;}
  cont.innerHTML=evs.map(function(ev){
    return '<div class="quick-ev-item"><div class="quick-ev-dot" style="background:'+(ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+'"></div><div class="quick-ev-title">'+ev.title+'</div><div class="quick-ev-actions"><div class="icon-btn" onclick="openEditEvent(\''+ev.id+'\')" title="แก้ไข">✏️</div><div class="icon-btn del" onclick="deleteEvConfirm(\''+ev.id+'\')" title="ลบ">🗑️</div></div></div>';
  }).join('');
}

/* ── ADD/EDIT EVENT ── */
function buildColorPicker(){
  var html='';
  for(var i=0;i<ACOLORS.length;i++){var c=ACOLORS[i];html+='<div class="color-opt'+(c===ADM.selectedColor?' selected':'')+'" style="background:'+c+'" onclick="selectColor(\''+c+'\',this)"></div>';}
  document.getElementById('colorPicker').innerHTML=html;
}
function selectColor(c,el){ADM.selectedColor=c;document.querySelectorAll('.color-opt').forEach(function(o){o.classList.remove('selected');});el.classList.add('selected');}
function syncCatColor(){var c=CATCOL[document.getElementById('ev_category').value];if(c){ADM.selectedColor=c;buildColorPicker();}}
function openAddEvent(){
  if(!ADM.selectedDate){showToast('กรุณาเลือกวันที่ก่อน','warning');return;}
  document.getElementById('evModalTitle').textContent='เพิ่มกิจกรรม';
  document.getElementById('ev_id').value='';document.getElementById('ev_title').value='';
  document.getElementById('ev_date').value=ADM.selectedDate;document.getElementById('ev_category').value='ทั่วไป';
  document.getElementById('ev_location').value='';document.getElementById('ev_description').value='';
  ADM.selectedColor='#4f6ef7';buildColorPicker();showModal('evModal');
}
function openEditEvent(id){
  var ev=null;for(var i=0;i<ADM.allEvents.length;i++){if(ADM.allEvents[i].id===id){ev=ADM.allEvents[i];break;}}
  if(!ev)return;
  document.getElementById('evModalTitle').textContent='แก้ไขกิจกรรม';
  document.getElementById('ev_id').value=ev.id;document.getElementById('ev_title').value=ev.title;
  document.getElementById('ev_date').value=ev.date||'';document.getElementById('ev_category').value=ev.category||'ทั่วไป';
  document.getElementById('ev_location').value=ev.location||'';document.getElementById('ev_description').value=ev.description||'';
  ADM.selectedColor=ev.color||'#4f6ef7';buildColorPicker();showModal('evModal');
}
function submitEvent(){
  var title=document.getElementById('ev_title').value.trim(),date=document.getElementById('ev_date').value;
  if(!title||!date){showToast('กรุณากรอกชื่อและวันที่','warning');return;}
  document.getElementById('evModalLoading').style.display='block';
  var id=document.getElementById('ev_id').value;
  var data={id:id,title:title,date:date,
    category:document.getElementById('ev_category').value,
    location:document.getElementById('ev_location').value,
    description:document.getElementById('ev_description').value,
    color:ADM.selectedColor,allDay:true,
    adminPass:'' /* ไม่ต้องกรอกรหัสผ่านซ้ำ — เข้าระบบมาแล้ว */
  };
  if(IS_GAS){
    var fn=id?'updateEvent':'addEvent';
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
        document.getElementById('evModalLoading').style.display='none';
        if(res&&res.success){
          showToast(id?'แก้ไขสำเร็จ':'เพิ่มกิจกรรมสำเร็จ','success');
          closeModal('evModal');
          /* อัพเดต local state ทันที ไม่ต้อง round-trip server */
          if(res.data) {
            var updEv = parseEv(res.data);
            if(id) {
              for(var ui=0;ui<ADM.allEvents.length;ui++){
                if(ADM.allEvents[ui].id===id){ ADM.allEvents[ui]=updEv; break; }
              }
            } else {
              ADM.allEvents.push(updEv);
            }
            PUB.allEvents = ADM.allEvents.slice();
            _rebuildPubIdx(); _rebuildAdmIdx(); _rebuildEvById();
          }
          renderAdminCalendar();
          if(ADM_VIEW_MODE==='month') renderAdminMonthView();
          if(ADM.selectedDate) renderQuickEvents(ADM.selectedDate);
          renderDayTable(); updateAdminBadge();
          /* sync กับ server ในพื้นหลัง */
          refreshAdminEvents();
        } else {
          showToast((res&&res.error)||'บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ','error');
        }
      })
      .withFailureHandler(function(err){
        document.getElementById('evModalLoading').style.display='none';
        showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: '+(err.message||''),'error');
      })
      [fn](data);
  } else {
    setTimeout(function(){
      document.getElementById('evModalLoading').style.display='none';
      if(!id){ADM.allEvents.push(Object.assign({},data,{id:'demo_'+Date.now(),_date:new Date(data.date)}));}
      else{for(var i=0;i<ADM.allEvents.length;i++){if(ADM.allEvents[i].id===id){ADM.allEvents[i]=Object.assign({},data,{_date:new Date(data.date)});break;}}}
      PUB.allEvents=ADM.allEvents.slice();
      showToast(id?'แก้ไขสำเร็จ':'เพิ่มกิจกรรมสำเร็จ','success');
      closeModal('evModal');renderAdminCalendar();if(ADM.selectedDate)renderQuickEvents(ADM.selectedDate);
    },300);
  }
}
function deleteEvConfirm(id){
  var ev=getEvById(id)||null;
  var ds=ev?ev.date:'';
  showConfirm('ลบกิจกรรม','ต้องการลบ "'+(ev?ev.title:'')+'" ใช่หรือไม่?',function(){
    /* ── แสดง loading บน quick-ev-item ที่กำลังลบ ── */
    var itemEl=document.querySelector('.quick-ev-item .icon-btn.del[onclick*="'+id+'"]');
    var cardEl=itemEl?itemEl.closest('.quick-ev-item'):null;
    if(cardEl){
      cardEl.style.opacity='0.45';
      cardEl.style.pointerEvents='none';
      /* แทนที่ actions ด้วย spinner */
      var actEl=cardEl.querySelector('.quick-ev-actions');
      if(actEl) actEl.innerHTML='<div class="loading-spin" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></div>';
    }
    if(IS_GAS){
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
          if(res&&res.success){
            showToast('ลบสำเร็จ','success');
            ADM.allEvents=ADM.allEvents.filter(function(e){return e.id!==id;});
            PUB.allEvents=ADM.allEvents.slice();
            if(ds){
              if(document.getElementById('evModal').classList.contains('show')) openDtDayModal(ds);
            }
            renderAdminCalendar();
            if(ADM_VIEW_MODE==='month') renderAdminMonthView();
            if(ADM.selectedDate) renderQuickEvents(ADM.selectedDate);
            renderDayTable();
            updateAdminBadge();
            _rebuildPubIdx(); _rebuildAdmIdx(); _rebuildEvById();
          } else {
            /* คืนสภาพ card ถ้า error */
            if(cardEl){ cardEl.style.opacity=''; cardEl.style.pointerEvents=''; }
            if(ADM.selectedDate) renderQuickEvents(ADM.selectedDate);
            showToast((res&&res.error)||'เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');
          }
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(){
          if(cardEl){ cardEl.style.opacity=''; cardEl.style.pointerEvents=''; }
          if(ADM.selectedDate) renderQuickEvents(ADM.selectedDate);
          showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');
        })
        .deleteEvent(id,'');
    } else {
      ADM.allEvents=ADM.allEvents.filter(function(e){return e.id!==id;});
      PUB.allEvents=ADM.allEvents.slice();
      showToast('ลบสำเร็จ','success');
      if(ds && document.getElementById('evModal').classList.contains('show')) openDtDayModal(ds);
      renderAdminCalendar();
      if(ADM.selectedDate) renderQuickEvents(ADM.selectedDate);
      renderDayTable();
    }
  });
}
function refreshAdminEvents(){
  if(!IS_GAS)return;
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
    if(res&&res.success){
      ADM.allEvents=(res.data||[]).map(parseEv);
      PUB.allEvents=ADM.allEvents.slice();
      _rebuildPubIdx(); _rebuildAdmIdx(); _rebuildEvById();
      renderAdminCalendar();
      if(ADM_VIEW_MODE==='month') renderAdminMonthView();
      if(ADM.selectedDate) renderQuickEvents(ADM.selectedDate);
      /* อัพเดตตารางวันเรียน + year plan ด้วย */
      renderDayTable();
      renderYearPlan();
      updateStats();
      updateAdminBadge();
    }
  }).withFailureHandler(function(){}).getEvents(null);
}

/* ── HOLIDAYS ── */
function importHolidays(){setupHolidayYears();document.getElementById('holidayList').innerHTML='';document.getElementById('holidayImportBtn').style.display='none';showModal('holidayModal');}
function setupHolidayYears(){
  var sel=document.getElementById('holidayImportYear');sel.innerHTML='';var y=new Date().getFullYear();
  for(var i=-1;i<=2;i++){var o=document.createElement('option');o.value=y+i;o.textContent='พ.ศ. '+(y+i+543);if(i===0)o.selected=true;sel.appendChild(o);}
}
function fetchHolidays(){
  var year=parseInt(document.getElementById('holidayImportYear').value);
  document.getElementById('holidayImportLoading').style.display='block';document.getElementById('holidayList').innerHTML='';document.getElementById('holidayImportBtn').style.display='none';
  if(IS_GAS){
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      document.getElementById('holidayImportLoading').style.display='none';
      if(!res||!res.data||!res.data.length){document.getElementById('holidayList').innerHTML='<div style="padding:14px;text-align:center;color:var(--text3);font-size:12px">ไม่พบข้อมูล</div>';return;}
      var exist={};ADM.allEvents.forEach(function(e){if(e.date)exist[e.date]=true;});
      ADM._newHolidays=res.data.filter(function(h){return !exist[h.date];});
      if(!ADM._newHolidays.length){document.getElementById('holidayList').innerHTML='<div style="padding:14px;text-align:center;color:var(--text3);font-size:12px">วันหยุดทั้งหมดมีอยู่แล้ว</div>';return;}
      var html='';for(var j=0;j<ADM._newHolidays.length;j++){var h=ADM._newHolidays[j];html+='<div class="holiday-item"><input type="checkbox" class="holiday-check" id="hol'+j+'" checked><div style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:#ef4444;flex-shrink:0"></div><div><div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700">'+h.title+'</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">'+fmtThai(new Date(h.date))+'</div></div></div>';}
      document.getElementById('holidayList').innerHTML=html;document.getElementById('holidayImportBtn').style.display='block';
    }).withFailureHandler(function(){document.getElementById('holidayImportLoading').style.display='none';showToast('ไม่สามารถดึงข้อมูลได้','error');}).getThaiHolidays(year);
  } else {setTimeout(function(){document.getElementById('holidayImportLoading').style.display='none';document.getElementById('holidayList').innerHTML='<div style="padding:14px;text-align:center;color:var(--text3);font-size:12px">Preview mode</div>';},800);}
}
function importSelectedHolidays(){
  var checks=document.querySelectorAll('.holiday-check'),sel=[];
  for(var i=0;i<ADM._newHolidays.length;i++){if(checks[i]&&checks[i].checked)sel.push(ADM._newHolidays[i]);}
  if(!sel.length){showToast('ไม่ได้เลือกรายการ','warning');return;}
  if(!IS_GAS){showToast('Preview mode','warning');closeModal('holidayModal');return;}
  closeModal('holidayModal');
  loadOverlay(true);
  /* ส่งทั้ง batch ครั้งเดียวผ่าน importHolidaysToSheet ใน Code.gs */
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      loadOverlay(false);
      if(res&&res.success){
        showToast('นำเข้าวันหยุด '+res.count+' รายการสำเร็จ','success');
        refreshAdminEvents();
      } else {
        showToast((res&&res.error)||'นำเข้าไม่สำเร็จ','error');
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(err){
      loadOverlay(false);
      showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: '+(err.message||''),'error');
    })
    .importHolidaysToSheet(sel,'');
}

/* ── TEACHERS ── */
function renderTeachers(){
  var list=document.getElementById('teacherList');
  if(!ADM.teachers.length){
    list.innerHTML='<div style="padding:36px;text-align:center;color:var(--text3)"><div style="font-size:36px;margin-bottom:10px">👩\u200d🏫</div><div>ยังไม่มีรายชื่อครู</div></div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML=ADM.teachers.map(function(t){
    return '<div class="teacher-card">'
      +'<div class="teacher-avatar">'+(t.name?t.name.charAt(0):'T')+'</div>'
      +'<div style="flex:1">'
      +'<div class="teacher-name">'+t.name+'</div>'
      +'<div class="teacher-email">'+(t.email||'ไม่มีอีเมล')+'</div>'
      +(t.department?'<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--accent);font-weight:600">'+t.department+'</div>':'')
      +'</div>'
      +(t.email
        ?'<span style="font-size:11px;background:rgba(34,197,94,.1);color:#22c55e;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:8px;font-weight:700">📧 รับแจ้งเตือน</span>'
        :'<span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">ไม่รับแจ้งเตือน</span>')
      +'<div style="display:flex;gap:4px;margin-left:6px">'
      +'<div class="icon-btn" onclick="openEditTeacher(\''+t.id+'\')" title="แก้ไข">✏️</div>'
      +'<div class="icon-btn del" onclick="deleteTeacherConfirm(\''+t.id+'\')" title="ลบ">🗑️</div>'
      +'</div>'
      +'</div>';
  }).join('');
}
function openAddTeacher(){
  document.getElementById('tc_id').value='';
  document.getElementById('tc_name').value='';
  document.getElementById('tc_email').value='';
  document.getElementById('tc_dept').value='';
  document.getElementById('teacherModalTitle').textContent='👩\u200d🏫 เพิ่มครู';
  showModal('teacherModal');
}
function openEditTeacher(id){
  var t=null;
  for(var i=0;i<ADM.teachers.length;i++){if(ADM.teachers[i].id===id){t=ADM.teachers[i];break;}}
  if(!t) return;
  document.getElementById('tc_id').value=t.id;
  document.getElementById('tc_name').value=t.name||'';
  document.getElementById('tc_email').value=t.email||'';
  document.getElementById('tc_dept').value=t.department||'';
  document.getElementById('teacherModalTitle').textContent='✏️ แก้ไขข้อมูลครู';
  showModal('teacherModal');
}
function submitTeacher(){
  var name=document.getElementById('tc_name').value.trim();
  if(!name){showToast('กรุณากรอกชื่อ','warning');return;}
  var id=document.getElementById('tc_id').value;
  var data={id:id,name:name,email:document.getElementById('tc_email').value,department:document.getElementById('tc_dept').value};
  var isEdit=!!id;
  if(IS_GAS){
    var btn=document.querySelector('#teacherModal .btn-primary');
    if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.innerHTML='<span style="display:inline-block;width:14px;height:14px;border:2px solid rgba(255,255,255,.4);border-top-color:white;border-radius:50%;animation:spin .6s linear infinite;vertical-align:middle;margin-right:6px"></span>กำลังบันทึก...';}
    if(isEdit){
      /* update: ลบเก่าแล้วเพิ่มใหม่ — GAS ไม่มี updateTeacher โดยตรง */
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(delRes){
          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(addRes){
              if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึก';}
              if(addRes&&addRes.success){
                showToast('แก้ไขข้อมูลครูสำเร็จ','success');
                closeModal('teacherModal');
                google.script.run
                  .withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(r&&r.success){ADM.teachers=r.data||[];renderTeachers();}})
                  .withFailureHandler(function(){})
                  .getTeachersCalendar('');
              } else showToast((addRes&&addRes.error)||'เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function(err){if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึก';}showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');})
            .addTeacherCalendar(data,'');
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err){if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึก';}showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');})
        .deleteTeacherCalendar(id,'');
    } else {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
          if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึก';}
          if(res&&res.success){
            showToast('เพิ่มครูสำเร็จ','success');closeModal('teacherModal');
            google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(r&&r.success){ADM.teachers=r.data||[];renderTeachers();}}).withFailureHandler(function(){}).getTeachersCalendar('');
          } else showToast((res&&res.error)||'เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(err){if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึก';}showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: '+(err.message||''),'error');})
        .addTeacherCalendar(data,'');
    }
  } else {
    if(isEdit){
      for(var i=0;i<ADM.teachers.length;i++){
        if(ADM.teachers[i].id===id){
          ADM.teachers[i]=Object.assign({},ADM.teachers[i],{name:data.name,email:data.email,department:data.department});
          break;
        }
      }
      showToast('แก้ไขข้อมูลครูสำเร็จ','success');
    } else {
      ADM.teachers.push({id:'tc_'+Date.now(),name:data.name,email:data.email,department:data.department,active:true});
      showToast('เพิ่มครูสำเร็จ','success');
    }
    renderTeachers();closeModal('teacherModal');
  }
}
function deleteTeacherConfirm(id){
  var tName='';for(var i=0;i<ADM.teachers.length;i++){if(ADM.teachers[i].id===id){tName=ADM.teachers[i].name;break;}}
  showConfirm('ลบรายชื่อ','ต้องการลบ "'+tName+'"?',function(){
    if(IS_GAS){google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(res&&res.success){showToast('ลบสำเร็จ','success');ADM.teachers=ADM.teachers.filter(function(x){return x.id!==id;});renderTeachers();}}).withFailureHandler(function(){}).deleteTeacherCalendar(id,'');}
    else{ADM.teachers=ADM.teachers.filter(function(x){return x.id!==id;});renderTeachers();showToast('ลบสำเร็จ','success');}
  });
}

/* ── EXPORT ── */
function setupYearSelects(){
  /* ใช้เฉพาะ clearYear และ holidayImportYear เท่านั้นแล้ว */
  var y=new Date().getFullYear();
  var ids=['clearYear','holidayImportYear'];
  for(var ki=0;ki<ids.length;ki++){
    var sel=document.getElementById(ids[ki]);if(!sel)continue;sel.innerHTML='';
    for(var i=-5;i<=5;i++){
      var o=document.createElement('option');o.value=y+i;o.textContent='พ.ศ. '+(y+i+543);if(i===0)o.selected=true;sel.appendChild(o);
    }
  }
  /* set default export range = ปัจจุบัน */
  var n=new Date(),y2=n.getFullYear(),m=n.getMonth();
  /* default: ปีการศึกษาปัจจุบัน */
  var startY=y2,startM=4; if(m<4){startY=y2-1;}
  document.getElementById('exportDateFrom').value=startY+'-05-01';
  document.getElementById('exportDateTo').value=(startY+1)+'-04-30';
}
function _initAcadYearSelect(){
  /* populate acadYearSelect เมื่อยังไม่มี options */
  var sel=document.getElementById('acadYearSelect');
  if(!sel||sel.options.length) return;
  var n=new Date(),cy=n.getFullYear(),cm=n.getMonth();
  var defYear=cm>=4?cy:cy-1; /* ปีการศึกษาปัจจุบัน */
  for(var i=-2;i<=4;i++){
    var o=document.createElement('option');
    var yr=defYear+i;
    o.value=yr; o.textContent='พ.ศ. '+(yr+543);
    if(i===0) o.selected=true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  }
}
function onAcadYearSelectChange(val){
  /* เมื่อเปลี่ยน dropdown → อัพเดต range และ highlight ปุ่ม */
  var yr=parseInt(val);
  var from=yr+'-05-01', to=(yr+1)+'-04-30';
  document.getElementById('exportDateFrom').value=from;
  document.getElementById('exportDateTo').value=to;
  document.querySelectorAll('#page-export .export-opt').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('selected');});
  var btn=document.getElementById('acadRangeBtn');
  if(btn) btn.classList.add('selected');
  _showExportLabel(from,to,'ปีการศึกษา '+(yr+543));
}
function _showExportLabel(from,to,prefix){
  var fl=document.getElementById('exportRangeLabel');
  if(!fl) return;
  var fp=from.split('-'),tp=to.split('-');
  var fromTh=parseInt(fp[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(fp[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(fp[0])+543);
  var toTh=parseInt(tp[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(tp[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(tp[0])+543);
  fl.textContent=(prefix?prefix+' — ':'')+fromTh+' – '+toTh;
  fl.style.display='block';
}
function setExportRange(type){
  var n=new Date(),y=n.getFullYear(),m=n.getMonth();
  var from,to,label='';
  _initAcadYearSelect(); /* init dropdown ถ้ายังไม่มี */
  if(type==='term1'){
    var ay=m>=4?y:y-1;from=ay+'-05-01';to=ay+'-10-31';
    label='เทอม 1 ปีการศึกษา '+(ay+543);
  } else if(type==='term2'){
    var ay2=m>=4?y:y-1;from=ay2+'-11-01';to=(ay2+1)+'-04-30';
    label='เทอม 2 ปีการศึกษา '+(ay2+543);
  } else if(type==='academic'){
    /* อ่านจาก dropdown */
    var sel=document.getElementById('acadYearSelect');
    var ay3=sel?parseInt(sel.value):(m>=4?y:y-1);
    from=ay3+'-05-01';to=(ay3+1)+'-04-30';
    label='ปีการศึกษา '+(ay3+543);
  } else { /* year */
    from=y+'-01-01';to=y+'-12-31';
    label='ปีปฏิทิน '+(y+543);
  }
  document.getElementById('exportDateFrom').value=from;
  document.getElementById('exportDateTo').value=to;
  document.querySelectorAll('#page-export .export-opt').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('selected');});
  event.target.classList.add('selected');
  _showExportLabel(from,to,label);
}
function setExportType(t,el){
  exportType=t;
  document.querySelectorAll('#page-export .export-opt').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('selected');});
  el.classList.add('selected');
}
function doExport(){
  var from=document.getElementById('exportDateFrom').value;
  var to=document.getElementById('exportDateTo').value;
  if(!from||!to){showToast('กรุณาเลือกช่วงวันที่','warning');return;}
  if(from>to){showToast('วันที่เริ่มต้นต้องน้อยกว่าวันที่สิ้นสุด','warning');return;}
  if(IS_GAS){
    loadOverlay(true);
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
        loadOverlay(false);
        if(res&&res.success){
          if(!res.data||!res.data.length){
            showToast('ไม่มีกิจกรรมในช่วงวันที่เลือก','warning');return;
          }
          buildPrintPage(res.data,from,to);
        } else showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');
      })
      .withFailureHandler(function(){loadOverlay(false);showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');})
      .getEventsByRange(from+'T00:00:00Z', to+'T23:59:59Z');
  } else {
    var filtered=ADM.allEvents.filter(function(e){return e.date>=from&&e.date<=to;});
    if(!filtered.length){showToast('ไม่มีกิจกรรมในช่วงวันที่เลือก','warning');return;}
    buildPrintPage(filtered,from,to);
  }
}
/* setClearType: overridden in NEW FEATURES section below */
function setClearType(t,el){ clearType=t; }
function buildPrintPage(events,from,to){
  /* from/to = yyyy-MM-dd string */
  var fp=String(from).split('-'),tp=String(to).split('-');
  var fromLabel=parseInt(fp[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(fp[1])-1]+' '+( parseInt(fp[0])+543);
  var toLabel  =parseInt(tp[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(tp[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(tp[0])+543);
  var typeLabel=fromLabel+' – '+toLabel;
  /* กรองตามช่วง */
  var filtered=events.filter(function(ev){return ev.date&&ev.date>=from&&ev.date<=to;});
  var monthMap={};
  filtered.forEach(function(ev){
    if(!ev.date)return;
    var p=String(ev.date).split('-'),yr=parseInt(p[0]),mo=parseInt(p[1])-1;
    var k=yr+'-'+('0'+mo).slice(-2);if(!monthMap[k])monthMap[k]={y:yr,m:mo,evs:[]};monthMap[k].evs.push(ev);
  });
  var sortedKeys=Object.keys(monthMap).sort();
  var blocks='';
  sortedKeys.forEach(function(key){
    var mo=monthMap[key];var rows='';
    var sortedEvs=mo.evs.slice().sort(function(a,b){return a.date<b.date?-1:a.date>b.date?1:0;});
    sortedEvs.forEach(function(e2){
      var dp=String(e2.date).split('-'),dayNum=parseInt(dp[2])||'?';
      rows+='<tr style="border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0">'
        +'<td style="padding:8px 12px;width:110px;color:#666;font-size:13px">'+dayNum+' '+TH_MONTHS[mo.m]+'</td>'
        +'<td style="padding:8px 4px"><div style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:'+(e2.color||'#4f6ef7')+'"></div></td>'
        +'<td style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:14px;font-weight:700">'+e2.title+'</td>'
        +'<td style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:12px;background:'+(e2.color||'#4f6ef7')+'15;color:'+(e2.color||'#4f6ef7')+';border-radius:4px">'+(e2.category||'')+'</td>'
        +'<td style="padding:8px 12px;font-size:12px;color:#888">'+(e2.location||'')+'</td></tr>';
    });
    blocks+='<div style="break-inside:avoid;margin-bottom:20px"><div style="background:#4f6ef7;color:#fff;padding:10px 16px;border-radius:10px 10px 0 0;font-weight:800;font-size:15px">'+TH_MONTHS[mo.m]+' '+(mo.y+543)+'</div><table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse">'+rows+'</table></div>';
  });
  var w=window.open('','_blank');
  w.document.write('<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>'+typeLabel+'</title>'
    +'<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Prompt:wght@400;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">'
    +'<style>*{-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact!important;print-color-adjust:exact!important}body{font-family:Prompt,sans-serif;padding:32px;color:#333;background:#fff}@media print{body{padding:16px}}</style>'
    +'</head><body>'
    +'<div style="text-align:center;margin-bottom:28px;border-bottom:3px solid #4f6ef7;padding-bottom:20px">'
    +'<div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:800;color:#1e2d6b"><a href="https://www.facebook.com/Treetased.Traiyotee" target="_blank" style="color:inherit;text-decoration:none;">ปฏิทินปฏิบัติงาน</a></div>'
    +'<div style="font-size:14px;color:#4f6ef7;margin-top:4px">'+typeLabel+' &bull; รวม '+filtered.length+' กิจกรรม</div></div>'
    +(blocks||'<p style="text-align:center;color:#999">ไม่มีข้อมูล</p>')
    +'<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();}<\/script>'
    +'</body></html>');
  w.document.close();
}
