function doClearData(){ /* stub — see override */ }

/* ── TRASH ── */
function loadTrash(){
  if(!IS_GAS){
    document.getElementById('trashList').innerHTML='<div style="padding:36px;text-align:center;color:var(--text3)"><div style="font-size:36px;margin-bottom:10px">🗑️</div><div>ถังขยะว่างเปล่า (Preview)</div></div>';
    document.getElementById('trashDeleteAllBtn').style.display='none';
    return;
  }
  document.getElementById('trashList').innerHTML='<div style="padding:20px;text-align:center"><div class="loading-spin"></div></div>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res){ renderTrash(res&&res.success?(res.data||[]):[]);})
    .withFailureHandler(function(){renderTrash([]);})
    .getTrash('');
}

function renderTrash(items){
  var list=document.getElementById('trashList');
  var btn=document.getElementById('trashDeleteAllBtn');
  if(!items.length){
    list.innerHTML='<div style="padding:36px;text-align:center;color:var(--text3)"><div style="font-size:36px;margin-bottom:10px">🗑️</div><div>ถังขยะว่างเปล่า</div></div>';
    if(btn) btn.style.display='none';
    return;
  }
  if(btn) btn.style.display='flex';
  /* ข้อ 4: แปลงวันที่ deletedAt เป็นรูปแบบไทย */
  function fmtDeletedAt(val){
    if(!val||val==='-') return '-';
    var s=String(val);
    /* รองรับทั้ง ISO string และ yyyy-MM-dd */
    var d;
    if(s.indexOf('T')>-1||s.indexOf(' ')>-1) d=new Date(s);
    else {var p=s.split(/[-/]/);d=(p.length===3)?new Date(parseInt(p[0]),parseInt(p[1])-1,parseInt(p[2])):new Date(s);}
    if(isNaN(d.getTime())) return s;
    return d.getDate()+' '+TH_MONTHS[d.getMonth()]+' '+(d.getFullYear()+543);
  }
  list.innerHTML=items.map(function(it){
    var dateLabel=fmtThai(it._date||(function(){var p=String(it.date||'').split('-');return p.length===3?new Date(parseInt(p[0]),parseInt(p[1])-1,parseInt(p[2])):new Date(it.date);}()));
    return '<div class="trash-item" id="trash-'+it.id+'">'
      +'<div style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:'+(it.color||'#4f6ef7')+';flex-shrink:0"></div>'
      +'<div class="trash-info">'
      +'<div class="trash-title">'+it.title+'</div>'
      +'<div class="trash-meta">'+dateLabel+' &bull; '+(it.category||'')+'</div>'
      +'<div class="trash-meta" style="color:var(--text3)">ลบเมื่อ: '+fmtDeletedAt(it.deletedAt)+'</div>'
      +'</div>'
      +'<div class="trash-actions" id="trash-actions-'+it.id+'">'
      +'<button class="btn-sm" onclick="restoreItem(\''+it.id+'\')">♻️ กู้คืน</button>'
      +'<button class="btn-sm danger" onclick="permDeleteItem(\''+it.id+'\')">🗑️ ลบถาวร</button>'
      +'</div></div>';
  }).join('');
}

function restoreItem(id){
  /* spinner ระหว่างกู้คืน */
  var actDiv=document.getElementById('trash-actions-'+id);
  if(actDiv) actDiv.innerHTML='<div class="loading-spin" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-width:2px"></div>';
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      if(res&&res.success){showToast('กู้คืนสำเร็จ','success');loadTrash();refreshAdminEvents();}
      else{showToast((res&&res.error)||'เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');loadTrash();}
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(){showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');loadTrash();})
    .restoreEvent(id,'');
}

function permDeleteItem(id){
  showConfirm('ลบถาวร','ต้องการลบถาวร? กู้คืนไม่ได้อีก!',function(){
    /* ข้อ 3: แสดง spinner บนการ์ดที่กำลังลบ */
    var card=document.getElementById('trash-'+id);
    if(card) card.style.opacity='0.4';
    var actDiv=document.getElementById('trash-actions-'+id);
    if(actDiv) actDiv.innerHTML='<span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px"><div class="loading-spin" style="width:14px;height:14px;border-width:2px"></div>กำลังลบ...</span>';
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
        if(res&&res.success){showToast('ลบถาวรสำเร็จ','success');loadTrash();}
        else{showToast((res&&res.error)||'เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');loadTrash();}
      })
      .withFailureHandler(function(){showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');loadTrash();})
      .permanentDelete(id,'');
  });
}

/* ข้อ 2: ลบทั้งหมดในถังขยะทีเดียว */
function deleteAllTrash(){
  showConfirm('ลบทั้งหมดถาวร','⚠️ ต้องการลบข้อมูลทั้งหมดในถังขยะ?\nกู้คืนไม่ได้อีก!',function(){
    loadOverlay(true);
    if(!IS_GAS){loadOverlay(false);showToast('Preview mode','warning');return;}
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
        loadOverlay(false);
        if(res&&res.success){
          showToast('ลบถังขยะทั้งหมด '+res.count+' รายการ','success');
          loadTrash();
        } else showToast((res&&res.error)||'เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');
      })
      .withFailureHandler(function(err){loadOverlay(false);showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');})
      .emptyTrash('');
  });
}

/* ── SETTINGS ── */
function loadSettingsForm(data){
  if(!data) return;
  var keys=['school_name','telegram_bot_token','telegram_chat_id','notify_days_before'];
  for(var i=0;i<keys.length;i++){
    var el=document.getElementById('st_'+keys[i]);
    if(el&&data[keys[i]]!==undefined) el.value=data[keys[i]];
  }
  /* term settings ย้ายไปอยู่ที่ "ตารางวันเรียน" แล้ว — sync DT_SETTINGS เท่านั้น */
  DT_SETTINGS = Object.assign(DT_SETTINGS||{}, data);
}

function initTermYearListener(){
  /* st_term_year element ถูกย้ายไปที่ "ตารางวันเรียน" แล้ว — function นี้เป็น no-op */
}

function doSaveSettings(){
  var schoolName=document.getElementById('st_school_name').value;
  /* term settings จัดการที่ "ตารางวันเรียน" แล้ว — Settings บันทึกเฉพาะข้อมูลทั่วไป */
  var settings={
    school_name:schoolName,
    telegram_bot_token:document.getElementById('st_telegram_bot_token').value,
    telegram_chat_id:document.getElementById('st_telegram_chat_id').value,
    notify_days_before:document.getElementById('st_notify_days_before').value
  };
  var saveBtn=document.querySelector('#page-settings .btn-primary');
  if(saveBtn){saveBtn.disabled=true;saveBtn.innerHTML='<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px"><div class="loading-spin" style="width:14px;height:14px;border-width:2px"></div>กำลังบันทึก...</span>';}
  if(IS_GAS){
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      if(saveBtn){saveBtn.disabled=false;saveBtn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึกการตั้งค่า';}
      if(res&&res.success){
        showToast('บันทึกการตั้งค่าสำเร็จ','success');
        if(schoolName) document.getElementById('schoolName').textContent=schoolName;
        /* sync DT_SETTINGS */
        for(var k in settings) DT_SETTINGS[k]=settings[k];
      } else showToast((res&&res.error)||'บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ','error');
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err){
      if(saveBtn){saveBtn.disabled=false;saveBtn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึกการตั้งค่า';}
      showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: '+(err.message||''),'error');
    }).saveSettings(settings,'');
  } else {
    if(saveBtn){saveBtn.disabled=false;saveBtn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึกการตั้งค่า';}
    if(schoolName) document.getElementById('schoolName').textContent=schoolName;
    for(var k2 in settings) DT_SETTINGS[k2]=settings[k2];
    showToast('บันทึกสำเร็จ (Preview)','success');
  }
}

/* ── ACADEMIC DAY TABLE ── */
var DT_SETTINGS = {}; /* cache settings สำหรับ day table */

function setupDtYearSelect(){
  /* ข้อ 5: ทั้ง 3 dropdowns sync กัน */
  var y=new Date().getFullYear()+543;
  var selIds=['dtAcademicYear','dtAcademicYear2'];
  for(var si=0;si<selIds.length;si++){
    var sel=document.getElementById(selIds[si]);
    if(!sel) continue;
    sel.innerHTML='';
    for(var i=-1;i<=3;i++){
      var o=document.createElement('option');
      o.value=y+i; o.textContent='ปีการศึกษา '+(y+i);
      if(i===0) o.selected=true;
      sel.appendChild(o);
    }
  }
}

/* sync 3 dropdowns เมื่อเลือก */
function syncDtYear(val){
  var ids=['dtAcademicYear','dtAcademicYear2'];
  for(var i=0;i<ids.length;i++){
    var s=document.getElementById(ids[i]);
    if(s) s.value=val;
  }
}
function onDtYearChange(){
  var v=document.getElementById('dtAcademicYear').value;
  syncDtYear(v);
  loadDtTermFields(parseInt(v));
  renderDayTable();
}
function onDtYear2Change(){
  var v=document.getElementById('dtAcademicYear2').value;
  syncDtYear(v);
  loadDtTermFields(parseInt(v));
  renderDayTable();
}


/* โหลด term fields จาก DT_SETTINGS ลงฟอร์ม */
function loadDtTermFields(yearBE){
  var prefix='ay'+yearBE+'_';
  var fields=['term1_start','term1_end','term2_start','term2_end'];
  for(var i=0;i<fields.length;i++){
    var el=document.getElementById('dt_'+fields[i]);
    if(el) el.value=(DT_SETTINGS&&DT_SETTINGS[prefix+fields[i]])||'';
  }
  /* ข้อ 3: อัพเดต info card */
  setTimeout(updateDtTermInfoCard,50);
}

/* ── ข้อ 3: สรุปวันเรียนใน info card ── */
function updateDtTermInfoCard(){
  var yearBE=parseInt(document.getElementById('dtAcademicYear2').value)||(new Date().getFullYear()+543);
  var prefix='ay'+yearBE+'_';
  var t1s=DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term1_start']||'';
  var t1e=DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term1_end']||'';
  var t2s=DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term2_start']||'';
  var t2e=DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term2_end']||'';
  var body=document.getElementById('dtTermInfoBody');
  if(!body) return;
  if(!t1s||!t1e||!t2s||!t2e){
    body.innerHTML='<span style="color:var(--text3);font-size:11px">กำหนดวันเปิด-ปิดภาคแล้วกด "บันทึก"</span>';
    return;
  }

  function countSchoolDays(from,to){
    var cnt=0;
    var cur=new Date(from),end=new Date(to);
    while(cur<=end){
      var ds=fmtD(cur),dw=cur.getDay();
      var dayEvs=getAdmEvs(ds);
      var isHol=dayEvs.some(function(e){return e.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||e.category==='วันหยุด';});
      var isMkup=dayEvs.some(function(e){return e.category==='เรียนชดเชย';});
      if(isMkup) cnt++;
      else if(dw!==0&&dw!==6&&!isHol) cnt++;
      cur=new Date(cur.getTime()+86400000);
    }
    return cnt;
  }

  function toDays(from,to){
    return Math.round((new Date(to)-new Date(from))/86400000)+1;
  }
  function thDate(ds){
    var p=ds.split('-');
    return parseInt(p[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(p[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(p[0])+543);
  }

  var d1=countSchoolDays(t1s,t1e);
  var d2=countSchoolDays(t2s,t2e);

  body.innerHTML=
    '<div style="display:grid;gap:8px">'
    +'<div style="background:#eef1fe;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px">'
      +'<div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:#4f6ef7;margin-bottom:4px">ภาคเรียนที่ 1</div>'
      +'<div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">'+(t1s?thDate(t1s):'?')+' – '+(t1e?thDate(t1e):'?')+'</div>'
      +'<div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:900;color:#3a57e8;margin-top:4px">'+d1+' วันเรียน</div>'
    +'</div>'
    +'<div style="background:#ecfdf5;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px">'
      +'<div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:#14b8a6;margin-bottom:4px">ภาคเรียนที่ 2</div>'
      +'<div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">'+(t2s?thDate(t2s):'?')+' – '+(t2e?thDate(t2e):'?')+'</div>'
      +'<div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:900;color:#0f766e;margin-top:4px">'+d2+' วันเรียน</div>'
    +'</div>'
    +'<div style="background:rgba(79,110,247,.06);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px;text-align:center">'
      +'<div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:var(--text3);margin-bottom:3px">รวมทั้งปี</div>'
      +'<div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:900;color:#4f6ef7">'+(d1+d2)+' วันเรียน</div>'
    +'</div>'
    +'</div>';
}

/* ข้อ 2: บันทึกด้วย loading state */
function saveDtTermSettings(){
  var yearBE=parseInt(document.getElementById('dtAcademicYear2').value)||(new Date().getFullYear()+543);
  var prefix='ay'+yearBE+'_';
  var fields=['term1_start','term1_end','term2_start','term2_end'];
  var newSettings={};
  for(var i=0;i<fields.length;i++){
    var el=document.getElementById('dt_'+fields[i]);
    if(el&&el.value) newSettings[prefix+fields[i]]=el.value;
  }
  /* เก็บไว้ใน DT_SETTINGS ด้วย */
  for(var k in newSettings) DT_SETTINGS[k]=newSettings[k];

  var btn=document.getElementById('dtSaveBtn');
  if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.innerHTML='<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px"><div class="loading-spin" style="width:14px;height:14px;border-width:2px"></div>กำลังบันทึก...</span>';}

  if(IS_GAS){
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
        if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึกและอัพเดตตาราง';}
        if(res&&res.success){
          showToast('บันทึกสำเร็จ','success');
          updateDtTermInfoCard();
          renderDayTable();
        } else showToast((res&&res.error)||'บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ','error');
      })
      .withFailureHandler(function(err){
        if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึกและอัพเดตตาราง';}
        showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด','error');
      })
      .saveSettings(newSettings,'');
  } else {
    if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.innerHTML='💾 บันทึกและอัพเดตตาราง';}
    showToast('บันทึกสำเร็จ (Preview)','success');
    updateDtTermInfoCard();
    renderDayTable();
  }
}

function showAdminTab(tab){
  document.querySelectorAll('.page-section').forEach(function(p){p.classList.remove('active');});
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(function(n){n.classList.remove('active');});
  document.getElementById('page-'+tab).classList.add('active');
  var navEl=document.getElementById('nav-'+tab);
  if(navEl) navEl.classList.add('active');
  if(tab==='trash') loadTrash();
  if(tab==='daytable'){
    setupDtYearSelect();
    /* ข้อ 2: โหลด settings ทุกครั้งที่เปิด tab เพื่อให้ข้อมูลล่าสุดเสมอ */
    document.getElementById('dayTableContainer').innerHTML='<div style="padding:24px;text-align:center"><div class="loading-spin"></div></div>';
    if(IS_GAS){
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
        if(res&&res.success){
          var raw=res.data||{};
          /* normalize term dates ทันทีหลังโหลด */
          function ndLoad(v){
            if(!v)return "";
            if(v instanceof Date)return v.getFullYear()+"-"+("0"+(v.getMonth()+1)).slice(-2)+"-"+("0"+v.getDate()).slice(-2);
            var s=String(v).trim(),m1=s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
            if(m1)return m1[1]+"-"+m1[2]+"-"+m1[3];
            var m2=s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/);
            if(m2)return m2[3]+"-"+("0"+m2[1]).slice(-2)+"-"+("0"+m2[2]).slice(-2);
            return s.substring(0,10);
          }
          Object.keys(raw).forEach(function(k){
            if((k.indexOf("_start")>-1||k.indexOf("_end")>-1)) raw[k]=ndLoad(raw[k]);
          });
          DT_SETTINGS=raw;
        }
        var yv=parseInt(document.getElementById("dtAcademicYear").value)||(new Date().getFullYear()+543);
        loadDtTermFields(yv);
        renderDayTable();
      }).withFailureHandler(function(){renderDayTable();}).getSettings("");
    } else {
      var yv=parseInt(document.getElementById('dtAcademicYear').value)||(new Date().getFullYear()+543);
      loadDtTermFields(yv);
      renderDayTable();
    }
  }
}

/* ── RENDER DAY TABLE ── */
function renderDayTable(){
  var container=document.getElementById('dayTableContainer');
  if(!container) return;

  var yearBE=parseInt(document.getElementById('dtAcademicYear').value)||(new Date().getFullYear()+543);
  var prefix='ay'+yearBE+'_';
  var yearCE=yearBE-543;

  var t1s=(DT_SETTINGS&&DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term1_start'])||'';
  var t1e=(DT_SETTINGS&&DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term1_end'])||'';
  var t2s=(DT_SETTINGS&&DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term2_start'])||'';
  var t2e=(DT_SETTINGS&&DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term2_end'])||'';
  if(!t1s) t1s=yearCE+'-05-01';
  if(!t1e) t1e=yearCE+'-10-31';
  if(!t2s) t2s=yearCE+'-11-01';
  if(!t2e) t2e=(yearCE+1)+'-04-30';

  /* lookup maps */
  var holSet={}, evMap={};
  for(var ei=0;ei<ADM.allEvents.length;ei++){
    var ev=ADM.allEvents[ei];
    if(!ev.date) continue;
    if(ev.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||ev.category==='วันหยุด') holSet[ev.date]=ev.title;
    if(!evMap[ev.date]) evMap[ev.date]=[];
    evMap[ev.date].push(ev);
  }
  /* ข้อ 3: เรียนชดเชย = วันหยุด/เสาร์/อาทิตย์ที่มี category เรียนชดเชย */
  var makeupSet={};
  for(var mi2=0;mi2<ADM.allEvents.length;mi2++){
    if(ADM.allEvents[mi2].category==='เรียนชดเชย') makeupSet[ADM.allEvents[mi2].date]=true;
  }

  /* normalize date string: รองรับ Date object, MM/DD/YYYY, YYYY-MM-DD */
  function normD(v){
    if(!v) return '';
    if(v instanceof Date) return v.getFullYear()+'-'+('0'+(v.getMonth()+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+v.getDate()).slice(-2);
    var s=String(v).trim();
    var m1=s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
    if(m1) return m1[1]+'-'+m1[2]+'-'+m1[3];
    var m2=s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/);
    if(m2) return m2[3]+'-'+('0'+m2[1]).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+m2[2]).slice(-2);
    return s.substring(0,10);
  }
  t1s=normD(t1s);t1e=normD(t1e);t2s=normD(t2s);t2e=normD(t2e);
  function isBreak(ds){return !(ds>=t1s&&ds<=t1e)&&!(ds>=t2s&&ds<=t2e);}
  function getDow(ds){return new Date(parseInt(ds.substring(0,4)),parseInt(ds.substring(5,7))-1,parseInt(ds.substring(8,10))).getDay();}
  function isSat(ds){return getDow(ds)===6;}
  function isSun(ds){return getDow(ds)===0;}
  function isWeekend(ds){var d=getDow(ds);return d===0||d===6;}
  function isHoliday(ds){return !!holSet[ds];}
  function isMakeup(ds){return !!makeupSet[ds];}
  /* ข้อ 3: วันเรียน = ไม่ใช่ break + (ไม่ใช่ weekend หรือ holiday หรือ เป็น makeup) */
  function isSchoolDay(ds){
    if(isBreak(ds)) return false;
    if(isMakeup(ds)) return true; /* ชดเชย นับเป็นวันเรียน */
    return !isWeekend(ds)&&!isHoliday(ds);
  }

  /* pre-number */
  var schoolNums={}, cnt=0;
  var cur=new Date(yearCE,4,1), endD=new Date(yearCE+1,3,30);
  while(cur<=endD){
    var cds=cur.getFullYear()+'-'+('0'+(cur.getMonth()+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+cur.getDate()).slice(-2);
    if(isSchoolDay(cds)){cnt++;schoolNums[cds]=cnt;}
    cur=new Date(cur.getTime()+86400000);
  }

  /* months: พ.ค. ถึง เม.ย. */
  var months=[];
  cur=new Date(yearCE,4,1);
  while(cur<=endD){
    var my=cur.getFullYear(),mm=cur.getMonth();
    if(!months.length||months[months.length-1].y!==my||months[months.length-1].m!==mm) months.push({y:my,m:mm});
    cur=new Date(cur.getTime()+86400000);
  }

  var todayStr=fmtD(new Date());
  var MAX_DAY=31;
  var totalSchoolDays=0;

  var html='<table class="dt-table"><thead><tr>';
  html+='<th>เดือน</th>';
  for(var d=1;d<=MAX_DAY;d++) html+='<th>'+d+'</th>';
  html+='<th>รวม</th></tr></thead><tbody>';

  for(var mi=0;mi<months.length;mi++){
    var mo=months[mi];
    var dim=new Date(mo.y,mo.m+1,0).getDate();
    var monthSchoolDays=0;
    html+='<tr><td><div class="dt-month-label">'+TH_MONTHS[mo.m]+' '+(mo.y+543)+'</div></td>';

    /* ── ข้อ 1: หา break spans ภายในแถวนี้ ──
       สร้าง array ของ cell info ก่อน แล้วค่อย merge */
    var cells=[];
    for(var day=1;day<=MAX_DAY;day++){
      if(day>dim){cells.push({type:'empty'});continue;}
      var ds=mo.y+'-'+('0'+(mo.m+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+day).slice(-2);
      var dayEvs=evMap[ds]||[];
      var brk=isBreak(ds);
      var wknd=isWeekend(ds);
      var hol=isHoliday(ds);
      var mkup=isMakeup(ds);
      var sat=isSat(ds);
      var sun=isSun(ds);
      var isTdy=(ds===todayStr);
      var sn=schoolNums[ds];
      var type, label='', title='', clickDs='';
      if(brk){ type='break'; }
      else if(mkup){ monthSchoolDays++;totalSchoolDays++; type='makeup'; label=sn?String(sn):'ช'; title=dayEvs.map(function(e){return e.title;}).join(', ')||'เรียนชดเชย'; clickDs=ds; }
      else if(hol){ type='hol'; label='ห'; title=holSet[ds]||'วันหยุด'; clickDs=ds; }
      else if(sat){ type='sat'; label='ส'; clickDs=ds; }
      else if(sun){ type='sun'; label='อ'; clickDs=ds; }
      else{
        /* วันเรียน */
        monthSchoolDays++;totalSchoolDays++;
        if(dayEvs.length>0){type='event'; title=dayEvs.map(function(e){return e.title;}).join(', ');}
        else type='school';
        label=sn?String(sn):'';
        clickDs=ds;
      }
      cells.push({type:type,label:label,title:title,clickDs:clickDs,isTdy:isTdy,ds:ds});
    }

    /* render cells — merge consecutive break cells */
    var ci=0;
    while(ci<cells.length){
      var cell=cells[ci];
      if(cell.type==='break'){
        /* count consecutive break cells */
        var bStart=ci, bEnd=ci;
        while(bEnd+1<cells.length && cells[bEnd+1].type==='break') bEnd++;
        var span=bEnd-bStart+1;
        /* ถ้า span เล็กกว่า 3 ไม่ merge */
        if(span>=3){
          /* ข้อ 1: merge cells - ใช้ colspan */
          var todayInBreak=false;
          for(var bi=bStart;bi<=bEnd;bi++){if(cells[bi].isTdy){todayInBreak=true;break;}}
          var cls='dc-break-td'+(todayInBreak?' dc-today':'');
          html+='<td colspan="'+span+'" class="'+cls+'" style="text-align:center;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.5px;color:#334155">ปิดภาคเรียน</td>';
          ci=bEnd+1;
        } else {
          /* span เล็ก render ทีละอัน */
          for(var bii=bStart;bii<=bEnd;bii++){
            var cls2='dc-break-td'+(cells[bii].isTdy?' dc-today':'');
            html+='<td class="'+cls2+'">ปิด</td>';
          }
          ci=bEnd+1;
        }
      } else {
        var td_cls='';
        if(cell.type==='empty') td_cls='dc-empty';
        else if(cell.type==='school') td_cls='dc-school-td';
        else if(cell.type==='event') td_cls='dc-event-td';
        else if(cell.type==='hol') td_cls='dc-hol-td';
        else if(cell.type==='sat') td_cls='dc-sat-td';
        else if(cell.type==='sun') td_cls='dc-sun-td';
        else if(cell.type==='makeup') td_cls='dc-makeup-td';
        if(cell.isTdy) td_cls+=' dc-today';
        var onclick_attr='';
        if(cell.clickDs){
          onclick_attr=' onclick="openDtDayModal(\''+cell.clickDs+'\')"';
        }
        var title_attr=cell.title?' title="'+cell.title.replace(/"/g,'&quot;')+'"':'';
        html+='<td class="'+td_cls+'"'+onclick_attr+title_attr+'>'+cell.label+'</td>';
        ci++;
      }
    }
    html+='<td class="dt-sum-col">'+monthSchoolDays+'</td></tr>';
  }
  html+='<tr style="background:rgba(79,110,247,.08)">';
  html+='<td style="font-weight:800;font-size:12px;color:#4f6ef7">รวมวันทั้งหมด</td>';
  html+='<td colspan="'+MAX_DAY+'" style="border:none;background:rgba(79,110,247,.08)"></td>';
  html+='<td class="dt-sum-col" style="font-size:14px;font-weight:900">'+totalSchoolDays+'</td>';
  html+='</tr>';
  html+='</tbody></table>';
  container.innerHTML=html;

  /* ── ข้อ 7: render หน้า public ด้วย ── */
  renderPubDayTable(yearBE,t1s,t1e,t2s,t2e);
  /* ── ข้อ 2: อัพเดตสรุปวันเรียนด้วยทุกครั้ง ── */
  setTimeout(updateDtTermInfoCard, 30);
}

/* ── ข้อ 7: ตารางหน้า public (อ่านอย่างเดียว) ── */

function renderPubDayTable(yearBE,t1s,t1e,t2s,t2e){
  var container=document.getElementById('pubDayTableBody');
  if(!container) return;
  var yearCE=yearBE-543;
  if(!t1s) t1s=yearCE+'-05-01';
  if(!t1e) t1e=yearCE+'-10-31';
  if(!t2s) t2s=yearCE+'-11-01';
  if(!t2e) t2e=(yearCE+1)+'-04-30';

  /* อัพเดต dropdown ให้ตรงกับ yearBE */
  var _pSel2=document.getElementById('pubDtYearSelect');
  if(_pSel2 && _pSel2.value!=yearBE) _pSel2.value=yearBE;

  var holSet={},evMap={};
  for(var ei=0;ei<PUB.allEvents.length;ei++){
    var ev=PUB.allEvents[ei]; if(!ev.date) continue;
    if(ev.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||ev.category==='วันหยุด') holSet[ev.date]=ev.title;
    if(!evMap[ev.date]) evMap[ev.date]=[];
    evMap[ev.date].push(ev);
  }
  var makeupSet={};
  for(var mi2=0;mi2<PUB.allEvents.length;mi2++){if(PUB.allEvents[mi2].category==='เรียนชดเชย') makeupSet[PUB.allEvents[mi2].date]=true;}

  /* normalize date string: รองรับ Date object, MM/DD/YYYY, YYYY-MM-DD */
  function normDP(v){
    if(!v) return '';
    if(v instanceof Date) return v.getFullYear()+'-'+('0'+(v.getMonth()+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+v.getDate()).slice(-2);
    var s=String(v).trim();
    var m1=s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
    if(m1) return m1[1]+'-'+m1[2]+'-'+m1[3];
    var m2=s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/);
    if(m2) return m2[3]+'-'+('0'+m2[1]).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+m2[2]).slice(-2);
    return s.substring(0,10);
  }
  t1s=normDP(t1s);t1e=normDP(t1e);t2s=normDP(t2s);t2e=normDP(t2e);
  function isBreakP(ds){return !(ds>=t1s&&ds<=t1e)&&!(ds>=t2s&&ds<=t2e);}
  function getDowP(ds){return new Date(parseInt(ds.substring(0,4)),parseInt(ds.substring(5,7))-1,parseInt(ds.substring(8,10))).getDay();}
  function isSchoolP(ds){
    if(isBreakP(ds)) return false;
    if(makeupSet[ds]) return true;
    var dw=getDowP(ds); return dw!==0&&dw!==6&&!holSet[ds];
  }
  var schoolNums={},cnt=0;
  var cur=new Date(yearCE,4,1),endD=new Date(yearCE+1,3,30);
  while(cur<=endD){
    var cds=fmtD(cur);
    if(isSchoolP(cds)){cnt++;schoolNums[cds]=cnt;}
    cur=new Date(cur.getTime()+86400000);
  }
  var months=[];
  cur=new Date(yearCE,4,1);
  while(cur<=endD){
    var my=cur.getFullYear(),mm=cur.getMonth();
    if(!months.length||months[months.length-1].y!==my||months[months.length-1].m!==mm) months.push({y:my,m:mm});
    cur=new Date(cur.getTime()+86400000);
  }
  var todayStr=fmtD(new Date());
  var MAX_DAY=31,totalSch=0;
  var html='<table class="dt-table"><thead><tr><th>เดือน</th>';
  for(var d=1;d<=MAX_DAY;d++) html+='<th>'+d+'</th>';
  html+='<th>รวม</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  for(var mi=0;mi<months.length;mi++){
    var mo=months[mi],dim=new Date(mo.y,mo.m+1,0).getDate(),mSch=0;
    html+='<tr><td><div class="dt-month-label">'+TH_MONTHS[mo.m]+' '+(mo.y+543)+'</div></td>';
    var cells2=[];
    for(var day=1;day<=MAX_DAY;day++){
      if(day>dim){cells2.push({type:'empty'});continue;}
      var ds=mo.y+'-'+('0'+(mo.m+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+day).slice(-2);
      var dayEvs=evMap[ds]||[];
      var brk=isBreakP(ds),dw=getDowP(ds),hol=!!holSet[ds],mkup=!!makeupSet[ds];
      var type,label='';
      if(brk){type='break';}
      else if(mkup){mSch++;totalSch++;type='makeup';label=String(schoolNums[ds]||'ช');}
      else if(hol){type='hol';label='ห';}
      else if(dw===6){type='sat';label='ส';}
      else if(dw===0){type='sun';label='อ';}
      else{mSch++;totalSch++;type=dayEvs.length?'event':'school';label=String(schoolNums[ds]||'');}
      var evTitle=(dayEvs||[]).map(function(e){return e.title;}).join(', ');
      cells2.push({type:type,label:label,isTdy:(ds===todayStr),title:evTitle,ds:ds,dayEvs:dayEvs});
    }
    var ci2=0;
    while(ci2<cells2.length){
      var cell2=cells2[ci2];
      if(cell2.type==='break'){
        var bs=ci2,be=ci2;
        while(be+1<cells2.length&&cells2[be+1].type==='break') be++;
        var sp=be-bs+1,tib=false;
        for(var bi2=bs;bi2<=be;bi2++){if(cells2[bi2].isTdy){tib=true;break;}}
        if(sp>=3){html+='<td colspan="'+sp+'" class="dc-break-td'+(tib?' dc-today':'')+'" style="text-align:center;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#334155">ปิดภาคเรียน</td>';}
        else{for(var bii2=bs;bii2<=be;bii2++) html+='<td class="dc-break-td'+(cells2[bii2].isTdy?' dc-today':'')+'">ปิด</td>';}
        ci2=be+1;
      } else {
        var tc2='dc-empty';
        if(cell2.type==='school') tc2='dc-school-td';
        else if(cell2.type==='event') tc2='dc-event-td';
        else if(cell2.type==='hol') tc2='dc-hol-td';
        else if(cell2.type==='sat') tc2='dc-sat-td';
        else if(cell2.type==='sun') tc2='dc-sun-td';
        else if(cell2.type==='makeup') tc2='dc-makeup-td';
        if(cell2.isTdy) tc2+=' dc-today';
        /* ข้อ 1: คลิกได้เมื่อมีกิจกรรม */
        var oc2='', tt2=cell2.title?(' title="'+cell2.title.replace(/"/g,'&quot;')+'"'):'';
        if(cell2.dayEvs&&cell2.dayEvs.length>0&&cell2.type!=='empty'){
          var evIds2=cell2.dayEvs.map(function(e){return e.id;}).join(',');
          oc2=' onclick="openPubDayModal(\''+cell2.ds+'\',\''+evIds2+'\')" style="cursor:pointer"';
        }
        html+='<td class="'+tc2+'"'+oc2+tt2+'>'+cell2.label+'</td>';
        ci2++;
      }
    }
    html+='<td class="dt-sum-col">'+mSch+'</td></tr>';
  }
  html+='<tr style="background:rgba(79,110,247,.08)">';
  html+='<td style="font-weight:800;font-size:12px;color:#4f6ef7">รวมวันทั้งหมด</td>';
  html+='<td colspan="'+MAX_DAY+'" style="border:none;background:rgba(79,110,247,.08)"></td>';
  html+='<td class="dt-sum-col" style="font-size:14px;font-weight:900">'+totalSch+'</td>';
  html+='</tr>';
  html+='</tbody></table>';
  container.innerHTML=html;
  var sec=document.getElementById('pubDayTableSection');
  if(sec) sec.style.display='block';
}

/* ── ข้อ 1: Modal สำหรับหน้า public (ดูอย่างเดียว) ── */
function openPubDayModal(ds,evIds){
  var ids=evIds.split(',');
  var evs=[];
  for(var i=0;i<ids.length;i++){
    for(var j=0;j<PUB.allEvents.length;j++){
      if(PUB.allEvents[j].id===ids[i]){evs.push(PUB.allEvents[j]);break;}
    }
  }
  if(!evs.length) return;
  if(evs.length===1){openEventModal(evs[0].id);return;}
  var parts=ds.split('-');
  var dateLabel=parseInt(parts[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(parts[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(parts[0])+543);
  document.getElementById('modalColorBar').style.background='#4f6ef7';
  document.getElementById('modalCatBadge').innerHTML='<span style="background:#eef1fe;color:#4f6ef7;padding:4px 12px;border-radius:14px;font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700">📆 '+dateLabel+'</span>';
  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent=evs.length+' กิจกรรมในวันนี้';
  document.getElementById('modalBody').innerHTML=evs.map(function(ev){
    return '<div class="modal-info-row" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="closeModal(\'eventModal\');setTimeout(function(){openEventModal(\''+ev.id+'\');},80)">'
      +'<div style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:'+(ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+';flex-shrink:0;margin-top:3px"></div>'
      +'<div><div class="modal-info-val" style="font-weight:700">'+ev.title+'</div>'
      +'<div class="modal-info-lbl">'+ev.category+'</div></div>'
      +'<span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text3)">›</span></div>';
  }).join('');
  showModal('eventModal');
}

/* ── Year Plan ── */
var PLAN_MODE='academic';

function setupPlanYearSelect(){
  var sel=document.getElementById('planYearSelect');
  if(!sel) return;
  sel.innerHTML='';
  var n=new Date(), cy=n.getFullYear(), cm=n.getMonth();
  var defYear=(cm>=3)?cy:cy-1;
  for(var i=defYear-3;i<=defYear+5;i++){
    var o=document.createElement('option');
    if(PLAN_MODE==='academic'){
      o.value=i; o.textContent='ปีการศึกษา '+(i+543);
      if(i===defYear) o.selected=true;
    } else {
      o.value=i; o.textContent='ปี พ.ศ. '+(i+543);
      if(i===cy) o.selected=true;
    }
    sel.appendChild(o);
  }
}




function onPlanYearSelect(){ renderYearPlan(); }

function switchPlanView(mode,el){
  PLAN_MODE=mode;
  document.querySelectorAll('#yearPlanSection .view-tabs .view-tab').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('active');});
  el.classList.add('active');
  setupPlanYearSelect();
  renderYearPlan();
}

function renderYearPlan(){
  var sel=document.getElementById('planYearSelect');
  if(!sel||!sel.options.length) setupPlanYearSelect();
  var selYear=sel?parseInt(sel.value):new Date().getFullYear();
  var months=[];
  if(PLAN_MODE==='academic'){
    months=mkMonths(selYear,4,8).concat(mkMonths(selYear+1,0,4));
  } else {
    months=mkMonths(selYear,0,12);
  }
  /* planPeriodLbl ไม่มีแล้ว (ใช้ dropdown แทน) */

  var evMap={};
  for(var ei=0;ei<PUB.allEvents.length;ei++){
    var ev=PUB.allEvents[ei]; if(!ev.date) continue;
    if(!evMap[ev.date]) evMap[ev.date]=[];
    evMap[ev.date].push(ev);
  }
  var holSet={};
  for(var hi=0;hi<PUB.allEvents.length;hi++){
    var hev=PUB.allEvents[hi];
    if(hev.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||hev.category==='วันหยุด') holSet[hev.date]=hev.title;
  }

  var todayStr=fmtD(new Date());
  var frag=document.createDocumentFragment();

  months.forEach(function(mo){
    var y=mo.y,m=mo.m;
    var dim=new Date(y,m+1,0).getDate();
    var firstDay=new Date(y,m,1).getDay();
    var prefix=y+'-'+('0'+(m+1)).slice(-2)+'-';

    /* เก็บกิจกรรมของเดือนนี้ เรียงตามวัน */
    var monthEvs=[];
    for(var d=1;d<=dim;d++){
      var ds=prefix+('0'+d).slice(-2);
      var dayEvs=evMap[ds]||[];
      for(var ei2=0;ei2<dayEvs.length;ei2++) monthEvs.push({ds:ds,d:d,ev:dayEvs[ei2]});
    }
    /* หาวันหยุดของเดือน */
    var monthHols=[];
    for(var d2=1;d2<=dim;d2++){
      var ds2=prefix+('0'+d2).slice(-2);
      if(holSet[ds2]) monthHols.push({d:d2,title:holSet[ds2]});
    }

    var block=document.createElement('div');
    block.className='yp-month-block';
    block.style.animation='fadeInUp .3s ease both';

    /* ── ซ้าย: mini calendar ── */
    var mcol=MCOLS[m%12];
    var wds='<div class="cal-weekdays" style="margin-bottom:3px">';
    ['อา.','จ.','อ.','พ.','พฤ.','ศ.','ส.'].forEach(function(w){wds+='<div class="cal-wd" style="font-size:.55rem">'+w+'</div>';});
    wds+='</div>';
    var dayCells='<div class="cal-days" style="gap:2px">';
    for(var pi=firstDay-1;pi>=0;pi--) dayCells+='<div class="cal-day other" style="border-radius:4px"><span class="cal-day-num" style="font-size:.6rem">&nbsp;</span></div>';
    for(var dd=1;dd<=dim;dd++){
      var dds=prefix+('0'+dd).slice(-2);
      var devs=evMap[dds]||[];
      var isT=(dds===todayStr),isHol2=!!holSet[dds];
      var dow=new Date(y,m,dd).getDay();
      var dcls='cal-day';
      if(isT) dcls+=' today'; else if(isHol2) dcls+=' holiday'; else if(dow===0) dcls+=' is-sun'; else if(dow===6) dcls+=' is-sat';
      var dots=devs.slice(0,3).map(function(e){return '<div class="cal-day-dot" style="background:'+(e.color||'#4f6ef7')+'"></div>';}).join('');
      dayCells+='<div class="'+dcls+'" onclick="openDayDetailFromPlan(\''+dds+'\')" style="border-radius:4px;cursor:pointer"><span class="cal-day-num" style="font-size:.68rem">'+dd+'</span><div class="cal-day-dots" style="height:3px">'+dots+'</div></div>';
    }
    var tot=Math.ceil((firstDay+dim)/7)*7;
    for(var ni=1;ni<=tot-firstDay-dim;ni++) dayCells+='<div class="cal-day other" style="border-radius:4px"><span class="cal-day-num" style="font-size:.6rem">&nbsp;</span></div>';
    dayCells+='</div>';

    /* ── ขวา: กิจกรรม ── */
    var evHtml='';
    /* ข้อ 5: ไม่แสดง hol chips ซ้ำ — รวมอยู่ใน monthEvs แล้ว */
    if(monthEvs.length>0){
      for(var ei3=0;ei3<Math.min(monthEvs.length,8);ei3++){
        var item=monthEvs[ei3];
        var dp=item.ds.split('-');
        var dLbl=item.d+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(dp[1])-1].substring(0,3)+'.';
        var c2=item.ev.color||'#4f6ef7';
        evHtml+='<div class="yp-ev-row">'
          +'<div class="yp-ev-dot" style="background:'+c2+'"></div>'
          +'<div class="yp-ev-date">'+dLbl+'</div>'
          +'<div class="yp-ev-info">'
          +'<div class="yp-ev-title">'+item.ev.title+'</div>'
          +'<span class="yp-ev-cat" style="background:'+c2+'18;color:'+c2+'">'+item.ev.category+'</span>'
          +'</div></div>';
      }
      if(monthEvs.length>8) evHtml+='<div style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--text3);padding-top:4px">+ อีก '+(monthEvs.length-8)+' กิจกรรม</div>';
    } else if(!monthHols.length){
      evHtml='<div class="yp-no-ev">ไม่มีกิจกรรม</div>';
    }

    block.innerHTML=
      '<div class="yp-cal-side">'
        +'<div class="yp-month-hd" style="justify-content:center;position:relative;margin-bottom:10px">'
          +'<span style="font-size:1.05rem;font-weight:800;color:'+mcol+'">'+TH_MONTHS[m]+'</span>'
          +'<span style="position:absolute;right:0;font-size:.72rem;color:var(--text3);font-weight:600">'+(y+543)+'</span>'
        +'</div>'
        +'<div class="yp-mini-cal">'
          +wds+dayCells
        +'</div>'
      +'</div>'
      +'<div class="yp-ev-side">'+'<div style="font-size:.75rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;margin-bottom:10px;padding-bottom:6px;border-bottom:2px solid var(--border)">กิจกรรมเดือนนี้</div>'+evHtml+'</div>';

    frag.appendChild(block);
  });
  var grid=document.getElementById('yearPlanGrid');
  grid.innerHTML='';
  grid.appendChild(frag);
}

function openDayDetailFromPlan(ds){
  var evs=(PUB.allEvents||[]).filter(function(e){return e.date===ds;});
  if(!evs.length) return;
  if(evs.length===1){openEventModal(evs[0].id);}
  else{openPubDayModal(ds,evs.map(function(e){return e.id;}).join(','));}
}



/* ── ข้อ 4: Day modal — ดู/เพิ่ม/แก้ไข/ลบกิจกรรมของวันนั้น ── */
function openDtDayModal(ds){
  var parts=ds.split('-');
  var dateLabel=parseInt(parts[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(parts[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(parts[0])+543);
  var dayEvs=[];
  for(var i=0;i<ADM.allEvents.length;i++){if(ADM.allEvents[i].date===ds) dayEvs.push(ADM.allEvents[i]);}

  /* ใช้ evModal (admin modal) โดยปรับ header */
  document.getElementById('evModalTitle').textContent='📅 '+dateLabel;
  document.getElementById('ev_id').value='';
  document.getElementById('ev_title').value='';
  document.getElementById('ev_date').value=ds;
  document.getElementById('ev_category').value='ทั่วไป';
  document.getElementById('ev_location').value='';
  document.getElementById('ev_description').value='';
  ADM.selectedDate=ds;
  ADM.selectedColor='#4f6ef7';buildColorPicker();

  /* แสดงกิจกรรมที่มีอยู่ด้านบน */
  var evListHtml='';
  if(dayEvs.length>0){
    evListHtml='<div style="background:var(--bg2);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;margin-bottom:14px">'
      +'<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text3);margin-bottom:8px;text-transform:uppercase">กิจกรรมวันนี้ ('+dayEvs.length+')</div>';
    for(var j=0;j<dayEvs.length;j++){
      var ev=dayEvs[j];
      evListHtml+='<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)">'
        +'<div style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:'+(ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+';flex-shrink:0"></div>'
        +'<div style="flex:1;font-size:12px;font-weight:600">'+ev.title+'</div>'
        +'<div style="display:flex;gap:4px">'
        +'<button style="padding:3px 8px;border-radius:6px;border:1.5px solid var(--border);background:white;font-size:10px;cursor:pointer" onclick="openEditEvent(\''+ev.id+'\')">✏️</button>'
        +'<button style="padding:3px 8px;border-radius:6px;border:1.5px solid rgba(239,68,68,.3);background:white;font-size:10px;cursor:pointer;color:var(--red)" onclick="deleteEvConfirm(\''+ev.id+'\')">🗑️</button>'
        +'</div></div>';
    }
    evListHtml+='</div>'
      +'<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text3);margin-bottom:8px;text-transform:uppercase;margin-top:10px">เพิ่มกิจกรรมใหม่</div>';
  }

  /* inject evList ก่อน form */
  var titleEl=document.getElementById('evModalTitle');
  /* ลบ evList เดิมออกถ้ามี */
  var oldList=document.getElementById('dtEvList');
  if(oldList) oldList.remove();
  var listDiv=document.createElement('div');
  listDiv.id='dtEvList';
  listDiv.innerHTML=evListHtml;
  titleEl.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',listDiv);

  showModal('evModal');
}

/* ── Export ตารางวันเรียน ── */
function exportDayTable(){
  var container=document.getElementById('dayTableContainer');
  if(!container||!container.querySelector('table')){showToast('ไม่มีตารางให้ Export','warning');return;}
  var yearBE=parseInt(document.getElementById('dtAcademicYear').value)||(new Date().getFullYear()+543);
  var prefix='ay'+yearBE+'_';
  var yearCE=yearBE-543;
  var t1s=(DT_SETTINGS&&DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term1_start'])||yearCE+'-05-01';
  var t1e=(DT_SETTINGS&&DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term1_end'])||yearCE+'-10-31';
  var t2s=(DT_SETTINGS&&DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term2_start'])||(yearCE+'-11-01');
  var t2e=(DT_SETTINGS&&DT_SETTINGS[prefix+'term2_end'])||((yearCE+1)+'-04-30');
  var title='ตารางวันเรียน ปีการศึกษา '+yearBE;

  /* ── ข้อ 3: rebuild table HTML จาก scratch แทนการ clone DOM ──
     เพื่อหลีกเลี่ยง undefined จาก onclick attribute ที่หลงอยู่ใน innerHTML */
  var holSet={},evMap={};
  for(var ei=0;ei<ADM.allEvents.length;ei++){
    var ev=ADM.allEvents[ei]; if(!ev.date) continue;
    if(ev.category==='วันหยุดราชการ'||ev.category==='วันหยุด') holSet[ev.date]=ev.title;
    if(!evMap[ev.date]) evMap[ev.date]=[];
    evMap[ev.date].push(ev);
  }
  var makeupSet={};
  for(var mi2=0;mi2<ADM.allEvents.length;mi2++){if(ADM.allEvents[mi2].category==='เรียนชดเชย') makeupSet[ADM.allEvents[mi2].date]=true;}

  /* normalize date string: รองรับ Date object, MM/DD/YYYY, YYYY-MM-DD */
  function normDE(v){
    if(!v) return '';
    if(v instanceof Date) return v.getFullYear()+'-'+('0'+(v.getMonth()+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+v.getDate()).slice(-2);
    var s=String(v).trim();
    var m1=s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
    if(m1) return m1[1]+'-'+m1[2]+'-'+m1[3];
    var m2=s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/);
    if(m2) return m2[3]+'-'+('0'+m2[1]).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+m2[2]).slice(-2);
    return s.substring(0,10);
  }
  t1s=normDE(t1s);t1e=normDE(t1e);t2s=normDE(t2s);t2e=normDE(t2e);
  function isBreakE(ds){return !(ds>=t1s&&ds<=t1e)&&!(ds>=t2s&&ds<=t2e);}
  function getDowE(ds){return new Date(parseInt(ds.substring(0,4)),parseInt(ds.substring(5,7))-1,parseInt(ds.substring(8,10))).getDay();}
  function isSchoolE(ds){
    if(isBreakE(ds)) return false;
    if(makeupSet[ds]) return true;
    var dw=getDowE(ds); return dw!==0&&dw!==6&&!holSet[ds];
  }
  var schoolNums={},cnt=0;
  var cur=new Date(yearCE,4,1),endD=new Date(yearCE+1,3,30);
  while(cur<=endD){
    var cds=fmtD(cur); if(isSchoolE(cds)){cnt++;schoolNums[cds]=cnt;}
    cur=new Date(cur.getTime()+86400000);
  }
  var months=[];
  cur=new Date(yearCE,4,1);
  while(cur<=endD){
    var my=cur.getFullYear(),mm=cur.getMonth();
    if(!months.length||months[months.length-1].y!==my||months[months.length-1].m!==mm) months.push({y:my,m:mm});
    cur=new Date(cur.getTime()+86400000);
  }
  var MAX_DAY=31, totalSch=0;
  /* ข้อ 3: header สีสัน — alternating gradient */
  var thColors=['#4f6ef7','#3b82f6','#6366f1','#8b5cf6','#a855f7','#ec4899'];
  var tblHtml='<table><thead><tr><th style="background:#1e2d6b;min-width:80px">เดือน</th>';
  for(var d=1;d<=MAX_DAY;d++){
    var hc=thColors[Math.floor((d-1)/5)%thColors.length];
    tblHtml+='<th style="background:'+hc+'">'+d+'</th>';
  }
  tblHtml+='<th style="background:#1e2d6b">รวม</th></tr></thead><tbody>';

  for(var mi=0;mi<months.length;mi++){
    var mo=months[mi],dim=new Date(mo.y,mo.m+1,0).getDate(),mSch=0;
    tblHtml+='<tr><td class="month-td">'+TH_MONTHS[mo.m]+' '+(mo.y+543)+'</td>';
    var cells=[];
    for(var day=1;day<=MAX_DAY;day++){
      if(day>dim){cells.push({type:'empty',label:''});continue;}
      var ds2=mo.y+'-'+('0'+(mo.m+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+day).slice(-2);
      var dw=getDowE(ds2),brk=isBreakE(ds2),hol=!!holSet[ds2],mkup=!!makeupSet[ds2];
      var sn=schoolNums[ds2];
      var type,label='';
      if(brk){type='break';}
      else if(mkup){mSch++;totalSch++;type='makeup';label=String(sn||'ช');}
      else if(hol){type='hol';label='ห';}
      else if(dw===6){type='sat';label='ส';}
      else if(dw===0){type='sun';label='อ';}
      else{mSch++;totalSch++;type=(evMap[ds2]&&evMap[ds2].length)?'event':'school';label=String(sn||'');}
      cells.push({type:type,label:label,ds:ds2});
    }
    var ci=0;
    while(ci<cells.length){
      var cell=cells[ci];
      if(cell.type==='break'){
        var bs=ci,be=ci;
        while(be+1<cells.length&&cells[be+1].type==='break') be++;
        var sp=be-bs+1;
        if(sp>=3){tblHtml+='<td colspan="'+sp+'" class="break-td" style="text-align:center;font-size:9px">ปิดภาคเรียน</td>';ci=be+1;}
        else{for(var bii=bs;bii<=be;bii++) tblHtml+='<td class="break-td">ปิด</td>';ci=be+1;}
      } else {
        var cls='';
        if(cell.type==='school') cls='school-td';
        else if(cell.type==='event') cls='event-td';
        else if(cell.type==='hol') cls='hol-td';
        else if(cell.type==='sat') cls='sat-td';
        else if(cell.type==='sun') cls='sun-td';
        else if(cell.type==='makeup') cls='makeup-td';
        /* ข้อ 3: ถ้า empty ไม่ออก undefined */
        tblHtml+='<td'+(cls?' class="'+cls+'"':'')+'>'+(cell.label||'')+'</td>';
        ci++;
      }
    }
    tblHtml+='<td class="sum-td">'+mSch+'</td></tr>';
  }
  tblHtml+='<tr class="total-row"><td>รวมวันทั้งหมด</td>';
  for(var di=0;di<MAX_DAY;di++) tblHtml+='<td></td>';
  tblHtml+='<td class="sum-td">'+totalSch+'</td></tr>';
  tblHtml+='</tbody></table>';

  /* ── ข้อ 4: กิจกรรมช่วงเปิดเทอม แยกภาค 1 + ภาค 2 ── */
  function buildEvSection(label,from,to){
    var items=[];
    var cf=new Date(from),ct=new Date(to);
    var scan=new Date(cf);
    while(scan<=ct){
      var sds=fmtD(scan);
      if(evMap[sds]){
        for(var i=0;i<evMap[sds].length;i++) items.push({ds:sds,ev:evMap[sds][i]});
      }
      scan=new Date(scan.getTime()+86400000);
    }
    if(!items.length) return '';
    var parts=from.split('-'),pe=to.split('-');
    var fromLbl=parseInt(parts[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(parts[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(parts[0])+543);
    var toLbl=parseInt(pe[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(pe[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(pe[0])+543);
    var html='<div class="ev-section"><div class="ev-section-title">'+label+'&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="ev-date-range">'+fromLbl+' – '+toLbl+'</span></div>';
    var curMonth='';
    for(var ii=0;ii<items.length;ii++){
      var item=items[ii];
      var mp=item.ds.split('-'),mKey=mp[0]+'-'+mp[1];
      if(mKey!==curMonth){
        if(curMonth!=='') html+='</div>';
        curMonth=mKey;
        html+='<div class="ev-month-group"><div class="ev-month-hd">'+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(mp[1])-1]+' '+(parseInt(mp[0])+543)+'</div>';
      }
      var dLbl=parseInt(mp[2])+' '+TH_MONTHS[parseInt(mp[1])-1];
      html+='<div class="ev-row"><span class="ev-day">'+dLbl+'</span>'
        +'<span class="ev-dot" style="background:'+(item.ev.color||'#4f6ef7')+'"></span>'
        +'<span class="ev-title">'+item.ev.title+'</span>'
        +(item.ev.category?'<span class="ev-cat">'+item.ev.category+'</span>':'')
        +'</div>';
    }
    if(curMonth!=='') html+='</div>';
    html+='</div>';
    return html;
  }

  var evSections=buildEvSection('ภาคเรียนที่ 1',t1s,t1e)+buildEvSection('ภาคเรียนที่ 2',t2s,t2e);

  var w=window.open('','_blank');
  w.document.write('<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>'+title+'</title>'
    +'<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Sarabun:wght@400;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">'
    +'<style>'
    +'*{box-sizing:border-box;-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact!important;print-color-adjust:exact!important}'
    +'body{font-family:Sarabun,sans-serif;padding:16px 20px;color:#1e2d6b;background:#fff;font-size:14px;}'
    +'h1{font-size:18px;font-weight:800;text-align:center;margin-bottom:12px;color:#1e2d6b;}'
    +'table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:11px;margin-bottom:20px;}'
    +'th{color:white;font-weight:700;padding:5px 2px;text-align:center;font-size:10px;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.2);}'
    +'td{border:1px solid #e2e8f0;padding:2px;text-align:center;height:22px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;}'
    +'.month-td{text-align:left;padding-left:5px;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;background:#f8fafc;border-right:2px solid #cbd5e1;}'
    +'.school-td{background:#dbeafe;color:#1d4ed8;}'
    +'.hol-td{background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c;}'
    +'.sat-td{background:#f3f0ff;color:#6d28d9;}'
    +'.sun-td{background:#ffe4e6;color:#be123c;}'
    +'.break-td{background:#f1f5f9;color:#64748b;font-style:italic;font-size:9px;}'
    +'.event-td{background:#bfdbfe;color:#1e40af;font-weight:800;}'
    +'.makeup-td{background:#d1fae5;color:#065f46;}'
    +'.sum-td{background:#eff6ff;font-weight:800;color:#1d4ed8;border-left:2px solid #bfdbfe;}'
    +'.total-row td{background:#eff6ff;font-weight:800;color:#1d4ed8;font-size:12px;}'
    +'/* กิจกรรม */'
    +'.ev-section{margin-bottom:16px;}'
    +'.ev-section-title{font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:#1e2d6b;padding:7px 12px;background:#eef1fe;border-left:4px solid #4f6ef7;border-radius:0 6px 6px 0;margin-bottom:8px;}'
    +'.ev-date-range{font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:#64748b;}'
    +'.ev-month-group{margin-bottom:8px;}'
    +'.ev-month-hd{font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#4f6ef7;margin-bottom:4px;padding-bottom:3px;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;}'
    +'.ev-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:3px 0;border-bottom:1px dotted #f0f0f0;}'
    +'.ev-day{min-width:70px;font-size:11px;color:#64748b;flex-shrink:0;}'
    +'.ev-dot{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;flex-shrink:0;}'
    +'.ev-title{flex:1;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;}'
    +'.ev-cat{font-size:10px;padding:1px 7px;border-radius:8px;background:#f1f5f9;color:#475569;}'
    +'@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:1cm;}'
    +'@media print{body{padding:0;}}'
    +'</style></head><body>'
    +'<h1>'+title+'</h1>'
    +tblHtml
    +(evSections?'<div style="margin-top:8px"><div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:800;margin-bottom:10px;color:#1e2d6b;border-bottom:2px solid #4f6ef7;padding-bottom:6px">📋 กิจกรรมช่วงเปิดเทอม</div>'+evSections+'</div>':'')
    +'<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();}<\/script>'
    +'</body></html>');
  w.document.close();
}



function doChangePass(){
  var np1=document.getElementById('newPass1').value,np2=document.getElementById('newPass2').value;
  if(!np1||!np2){showToast('กรุณากรอกรหัสผ่านใหม่ทั้ง 2 ช่อง','warning');return;}
  if(np1!==np2){showToast('รหัสผ่านใหม่ทั้ง 2 ช่องไม่ตรงกัน','error');return;}
  if(np1.length<4){showToast('รหัสผ่านต้องมีอย่างน้อย 4 ตัวอักษร','warning');return;}
  if(IS_GAS){
    /* ส่ง oldPass='' เพราะ changePassword ใน Code.gs รับ skip ได้ถ้าผ่าน session */
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      if(res&&res.success){showToast('เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่านสำเร็จ','success');document.getElementById('newPass1').value='';document.getElementById('newPass2').value='';}
      else showToast((res&&res.error)||'เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่านไม่สำเร็จ','error');
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err){showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาด: '+(err.message||''),'error');}).changePasswordDirect(np1);
  } else {showToast('เปลี่ยนรหัสผ่านสำเร็จ (Preview)','success');document.getElementById('newPass1').value='';document.getElementById('newPass2').value='';}
}
function toggleNewPass(n){
  var inp=document.getElementById('newPass'+n);
  var btn=document.getElementById('newPassEye'+n);
  if(inp.type==='password'){inp.type='text';btn.innerHTML=eyeOffSVG();}
  else{inp.type='password';btn.innerHTML=eyeOnSVG();}
}
function eyeOnSVG(){return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M1 12s4-8 11-8 11 8 11 8-4 8-11 8-11-8-11-8z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/></svg>';}
function eyeOffSVG(){return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M17.94 17.94A10.07 10.07 0 0 1 12 20c-7 0-11-8-11-8a18.45 18.45 0 0 1 5.06-5.94M9.9 4.24A9.12 9.12 0 0 1 12 4c7 0 11 8 11 8a18.5 18.5 0 0 1-2.16 3.19m-6.72-1.07a3 3 0 1 1-4.24-4.24"/><line x1="1" y1="1" x2="23" y2="23"/></svg>';}

/* ════════════════════════════════════════
   SHARED UTILS
════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* แปลงค่าใดก็ได้ → string "yyyy-MM-dd" */
function fmtD(d){
  if(!d) return '';
  if(typeof d==='string'){
    /* ถ้าเป็น yyyy-MM-dd อยู่แล้ว */
    if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d)) return d;
    /* ISO string หรือรูปแบบอื่น */
    d = new Date(d);
  }
  if(!(d instanceof Date)||isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
  return d.getFullYear()+'-'+('0'+(d.getMonth()+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+d.getDate()).slice(-2);
}

/* แปลง event object จาก server ให้ _date ถูกต้อง (local time ไม่ UTC shift) */
function parseEv(e){
  var ds = (typeof e.date==='string') ? e.date
    : (e.date instanceof Date) ? fmtD(e.date) : String(e.date||'');
  /* สร้าง Date แบบ local เพื่อไม่ให้ timezone offset ทำให้วันเลื่อน */
  var dt;
  var m = ds.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
  if(m) dt = new Date(parseInt(m[1]),parseInt(m[2])-1,parseInt(m[3]));
  else dt = new Date(ds);
  return Object.assign({},e,{date:ds,_date:dt});
}

/* ── Speed: pre-built index สำหรับ O(1) lookup แทน O(n) filter ── */
var _pubIdx={}, _admIdx={}, _evById={};

function _rebuildPubIdx(){
  _pubIdx={};
  PUB.allEvents.forEach(function(e){ if(!_pubIdx[e.date]) _pubIdx[e.date]=[]; _pubIdx[e.date].push(e); });
}
function _rebuildAdmIdx(){
  _admIdx={};
  ADM.allEvents.forEach(function(e){ if(!_admIdx[e.date]) _admIdx[e.date]=[]; _admIdx[e.date].push(e); });
}
function _rebuildEvById(){
  _evById={};
  ADM.allEvents.forEach(function(e){ _evById[e.id]=e; });
  PUB.allEvents.forEach(function(e){ if(!_evById[e.id]) _evById[e.id]=e; });
}
function getPubEvs(ds){ return _pubIdx[ds]||[]; }
function getAdmEvs(ds){ return _admIdx[ds]||[]; }
function getEvById(id){ return _evById[id]||null; }
function fmtThai(d){
  if(!(d instanceof Date)||isNaN(d.getTime())) return '-';
  return d.getDate()+' '+TH_MONTHS[d.getMonth()]+' '+(d.getFullYear()+543);
}
function loadOverlay(show){document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').classList[show?'add':'remove']('show');}
function showModal(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.add('show');}
function closeModal(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('show');}
document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay').forEach(function(o){o.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target===o)closeModal(o.id);});});
document.getElementById('loginModal').addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target===document.getElementById('loginModal'))closeLoginModal();});
function showConfirm(title,desc,onOk){
  document.getElementById('confirmTitle').textContent=title;document.getElementById('confirmDesc').textContent=desc;
  document.getElementById('confirmOkBtn').onclick=function(){closeModal('confirmModal');onOk();};showModal('confirmModal');
}
function showToast(msg,type){
  type=type||'info';var c=document.getElementById('toastContainer'),t=document.createElement('div');
  var icons={success:'✅',error:'❌',info:'ℹ️',warning:'⚠️'};
  t.className='toast '+type;t.innerHTML='<span>'+icons[type]+'</span><span>'+msg+'</span>';
  c.appendChild(t);requestAnimationFrame(function(){requestAnimationFrame(function(){t.classList.add('show');});});
  setTimeout(function(){t.classList.remove('show');setTimeout(function(){t.remove();},400);},3500);
}
document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Escape'){document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay.show').forEach(function(m){m.classList.remove('show');});closeLoginModal();}});
